# Lore notes

Every fact we rely on that came from outside the brief, with its source. Allowed sources: warcraft.wiki.gg and Blizzard's official sites. Anything a page labels as speculation is not canon. Zone lore sheets are added here before any dialogue is written for that zone.

The Skyborne lore brief (section 10 of the build prompt) is the only source for Skyborne facts. Don't extend it from elsewhere without recording the source here.

## Forever: setting

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Forever is set indefinitely in the first year of World of Warcraft (25 ADP): after Warcraft III Reforged: *Forsaken Kingdom*, before Molten Core | [warcraft.wiki.gg: World of Warcraft: Forever](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Patch_1.60) |
| Forever is not in the same continuity as retail WoW (a "time bubble", per the developers) | same page, citing a 2026-09-13 developer stream |
| Internal development name: *Camelot* | same page; [Patch 1.60.1/API changes/notes](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Patch_1.60.1/API_changes/notes) |

## Northshire Valley (partial; the full lore sheet comes in M3)

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Marshal (Douglas) McBride: human, a marshal of the Stormwind Army, commands Northshire Abbey's garrison and the valley's defense | [warcraft.wiki.gg: Marshal McBride](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Marshal_McBride) |
| He was concerned about kobolds infesting Northshire's woods and moving into Echo Ridge Mine, and marshaled Silver Hand paladins, warriors and other recruits against them | same page |
| His quests in original WoW: A Threat Within (turn-in), Kobold Camp Cleanup (kill 8 Kobold Vermin), Investigate Echo Ridge, Skirmish at Echo Ridge (kill 8 Kobold Laborers), Report to Goldshire, and the human class letters. **Unconfirmed for Forever.** | same page; [Kobold Camp Cleanup](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Kobold_Camp_Cleanup); [Skirmish at Echo Ridge](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Skirmish_at_Echo_Ridge) |
| Skirmish at Echo Ridge's completion has McBride admit there may still be kobolds in the mine and say he'll send others (paraphrased, not for dialogue) | [Skirmish at Echo Ridge](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Skirmish_at_Echo_Ridge) |

Unsure, needs capture or a source before use: whether Forever changed any Northshire quests; whether Human Hunters get a class letter from McBride; the exact location of the Defias in Northshire (believed to be near the vineyards in original WoW, but not yet sourced).

## Lore sheet: Northshire Valley and Goldshire (vertical slice, M3)

Sources: [Elwynn Forest questing guide](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Elwynn_Forest_questing_guide), [Elwynn Forest (Classic) quests](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Elwynn_Forest_(Classic)_quests), [Elwynn Forest (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Elwynn_Forest_(Classic)), [Marshal Dughan](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Marshal_Dughan), [Innkeeper Farley](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Innkeeper_Farley), [Lion's Pride Inn](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lion's_Pride_Inn), [Goldshire](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Goldshire), [The Collector (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Collector_(Classic)), [Further Concerns](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Further_Concerns), [Wanted: "Hogger"](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Wanted:_%22Hogger%22), [Report to Goldshire](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Report_to_Goldshire). All are original-WoW (Classic) facts; **Forever may change quests**, so every quest ID comes from capture, never from these pages.

**Key NPCs and what they care about (from their quests)**
- Marshal McBride (Northshire Abbey): commands the garrison; kobolds in the woods and Echo Ridge Mine; sends recruits on to Marshal Dughan.
- Deputy Willem (outside the Abbey): first face for new recruits (A Threat Within); the Defias thieves in the valley (Brotherhood of Thieves, Bounty on Garrick Padfoot); sends people to Eagan Peltskinner and Milly Osworth.
- Eagan Peltskinner: wolves across the border (Wolves Across the Border).
- Milly Osworth: her vineyard harvest taken by the Defias (Milly's Harvest); the Grape Manifest goes to Brother Neals.
- Marshal Dughan (Goldshire): the law in Elwynn outside Northshire; the kobold mines (Fargodeep, Jasperlode), Westbrook Garrison, the murloc rumors (Further Concerns, after Remy's A Fishy Peril), the Hogger bounty, The Collector and Manhunt, sending people on to Gryan Stoutmantle in Westfall.
- Innkeeper Farley (Lion's Pride Inn): family inn since King Llane I's time; hospitality; Falkhaan Isenstrider's best friend (Rest and Relaxation); later the Legend of Stalvan (Stalvan stayed at the inn).
- Remy "Two Times" (Goldshire): buys gold dust from kobold mines (Gold Dust Exchange); heard the murloc rumors (A Fishy Peril).
- William Pestle (in the inn): kobold candles (Kobold Candles, Shipment to Stormwind); helps the young lovers Maybell Maclure and Tommy Joe Stonefield (Collecting Kelp, The Escape).
- Smith Argus <Journeyman Blacksmith>: Elmore's Task; later Return to Verner.
- Melika Isenstrider <Assistant Innkeeper> (Lion's Pride Inn, main room): no quests in the live game (in the original beta she ended Rest and Relaxation); a relative of Falkhaan Isenstrider, relation unstated, so never specify it. No gossip menu in Forever (user). Source: [Melika Isenstrider](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Melika_Isenstrider).

**Storylines**
- *Northshire troubles:* kobolds (McBride), wolves (Eagan), Defias thieves in the vineyards (Willem, Milly), then Report to Goldshire.
- *Mines and kobolds:* Fargodeep Mine, then Jasperlode Mine (Dughan); Kobold Candles (Pestle) and Gold Dust Exchange (Remy) on the side.
- *Maclures vs Stonefields:* Tommy Joe and Maybell's romance; William Pestle's potion helps them (The Escape).
- *A Fishy Peril:* Remy's murloc rumor, then Dughan's Further Concerns, then Guard Thomas at Eastvale. The rumor proves true (lost guards, Rolf's fate).
- *Gnolls and the Collector:* the Gold Pickup Schedule from gnolls shows gold from Elwynn's mines going to "the Collector" near the Brackwell Pumpkin Patch. Dughan's Manhunt follows. The wiki notes this links the gnolls to the Defias.
- *Hogger:* a huge gnoll in southwestern Elwynn with a bounty from the Stormwind Army; proof goes to Dughan.

**Year 1 status (Forever):** after the Forsaken Kingdom campaign; Defias active; VanCleef not yet defeated by default (the Deadmines are a dungeon the player may or may not have done); no Cataclysm-era Northshire (no worgen, no Blackrock invasion of the Abbey). Not used: Dughan's Emerald Nightmare dream (*Stormrage*, much later).

**Unsure / to confirm with capture:** whether Forever changed any of these quests; Brother Danil, Llane Beshere, Brother Sammuel and Brother Neals still in place; whether Goldshire has children NPCs; the exact names of guard NPCs in Northshire and Goldshire; Remy's in-game greeting (his voice is written from his quests and nickname, and should be checked against captured text).

## Elwynn additions (Elwynn pack 1.2.0)

All original-WoW (Classic) facts; Forever may change quests, so quest IDs come from capture only.

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Brother Wilhelm: human paladin, Paladin Trainer, Goldshire behind the blacksmith; gives Tome of Divinity (human paladins, level 12), which sends you to Duthorian Rall in Stormwind | [Brother Wilhelm](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Brother_Wilhelm) |
| The Tome of Divinity belonged to Northshire Abbey's clerics; Turok's ogres took it in the First War; Anduin Lothar's first rescue attempt in the Deadmines failed and he had to be freed; the tome went back to the Abbey; Silver Hand trainees in Stormwind and Ironforge use it | [Tome of Divinity](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Tome_of_Divinity) |
| Duthorian Rall: human paladin, Knight of the Silver Hand, Cathedral of Light; tests paladins sent by Brother Wilhelm and Brandur Ironhammer through charity and penance; a demanding mentor; Tome of Divinity chain ends in Redemption | [Duthorian Rall](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Duthorian_Rall) |
| Falkhaan Isenstrider: at the exit of Northshire Valley; Innkeeper Farley is his best friend; related to Melika Isenstrider; Rest and Relaxation sends worn-out adventurers south to the Lion's Pride | [Falkhaan Isenstrider](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Falkhaan_Isenstrider); [Rest and Relaxation](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Rest_and_Relaxation) |
| Maybell Maclure (Maclure Vineyards) and Tommy Joe Stonefield (riverbank by Stonefield Farm) are in love; their families are bitter enemies; Gramma Stonefield and William Pestle help with Invisibility Liquor so she can slip away; she feels guilty about deceiving her family. Quests: A Visit With Maybell, Young Lovers, Speak with Gramma, Note to William, Collecting Kelp, The Escape | [Maybell Maclure](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Maybell_Maclure); [Tommy Joe Stonefield](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Tommy_Joe_Stonefield); [The Escape (Alliance)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Escape_(Alliance)) |
| Gramma Stonefield: oldest living Stonefield; thinks the feud damaging and pointless; wants Tommy Joe with Maybell; old friend of William Pestle. The feud's cause is not given anywhere: never state it | [Gramma Stonefield](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Gramma_Stonefield) |
| Guard Thomas: bridge west of Eastvale Logging Camp; Stormwind Army; formal. Quests: Further Concerns, Find the Lost Guards, Discover Rolf's Fate, Report to Thomas, Protect the Frontier; in Classic also Deliver Thomas' Report and Report to Gryan Stoutmantle | [Guard Thomas](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Guard_Thomas) |
| The lost footmen are Rolf Hartford and Malakai Stone, sent north along the river; Malakai's body found with his medallion; webbed tracks along Stone Cairn Lake lead toward a murloc village; Rolf's corpse identified by his medallion. How they died is not stated | [Find the Lost Guards](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Find_the_Lost_Guards); [Discover Rolf's Fate](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Discover_Rolf's_Fate) |
| Protect the Frontier: animals grow bolder the further from Goldshire; wolves and bears attack the Eastvale camp | [Protect the Frontier](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Protect_the_Frontier) |
| Marshal Haggard: retired former leader of the Stormwind Guard; manor south-east of the Eastvale camp, once a Flintridge summer cottage; failing eyesight; old papers include a Stalvan Mistmantle journal page; Dead-Tooth Jack (Defias) tricked him out of his marshal badge by posing as an official in the dark; drank with his soldiers at the Scarlet Raven, Darkshire; keeps his old kit in good order | [Marshal Haggard](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Marshal_Haggard) |
| Supervisor Raelen: centre of Eastvale Logging Camp; oversees quotas; strict and impatient. A Bundle of Trouble: eight bundles; lumber deadline; wolves and bears chased workers from cut wood; she asked Deputy Rainer, who is at Westbrook | [Supervisor Raelen](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Supervisor_Raelen); [A Bundle of Trouble](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Bundle_of_Trouble) |
| Theocritus: top floor of the Tower of Azora, which he owns; Mage of Tower Azora; eccentric, mostly uninvolved in everyday affairs, but opposes Morganth out of duty; gnomish servants; clothier Morley Eberlein. Quests: Theocritus' Retrieval, A Watchful Eye, Morganth | [Theocritus](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Theocritus) |
| Morganth: fallen mage in the Tower of Ilgalar, Redridge; once a student of Stormwind's mages; banished for dark magic; took *Ur's Treatise on Shadow Magic*; commands Shadowhide gnolls; Theocritus studies their pendants and uses the Eye of Azora | [Morganth](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Morganth) |

Not used (later eras): Wilhelm and Rall in the Legion-era Silver Hand; the Maclure-Stonefield family in a Draenor garrison; Haggard sending people to Westfall (Cataclysm); Theocritus and Cote Metcalf (Legion). Speculation not used: Wilhelm's link to the Brotherhood of Northshire or Councilman Wilhelm; who lives in Haggard's house.

## Capitals and their people (World pack role pools)

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Stormwind was destroyed by the Horde in the First War and rebuilt by the Stonemasons' Guild after the Second War; the unpaid stonemasons rioted and became the Defias | [Stormwind City](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stormwind_City); [Defias Brotherhood](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Defias_Brotherhood) |
| Year 1 Stormwind: Highlord Bolvar Fordragon is Regent and commander for ten-year-old King Anduin; King Varian vanished on the way to a summit at Theramore; Lady Katrana Prestor advises. Districts: Trade District, Cathedral Square, Old Town, Dwarven District, Mage Quarter, the Canals, Stormwind Keep, Valley of Heroes; the Stormwind City Guard keeps the law | [Stormwind City](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stormwind_City); [Bolvar Fordragon](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Bolvar_Fordragon); [Anduin Wrynn](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Anduin_Wrynn) |
| Ironforge: rings around the Great Forge at its centre; the Commons by the gate, Military Ward, Mystic Ward, Tinker Town, Hall of Explorers; the Deeprun Tram leaves from Tinker Town for Stormwind; King Magni Bronzebeard rules before the Cataclysm | [Ironforge](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Ironforge) |
| Darnassus: Teldrassil was planted after the Third War by druids under Fandral Staghelm; Warrior's, Craftsmen's and Tradesmen's Terraces, the Temple of the Moon, the Cenarion Enclave; Tyrande Whisperwind leads; Sentinels guard it | [Darnassus](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Darnassus) |
| Orgrimmar: valleys on Durotar's northern edge, named for Orgrim Doomhammer; Valley of Strength, the Drag; in original WoW Thrall held court in Grommash Hold | [Orgrimmar](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Orgrimmar); [Grommash Hold](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Grommash_Hold) |
| Thunder Bluff: mesas in northern Mulgore; Elder Rise, Hunter Rise, Spirit Rise joined by rope bridges; tauren-made lifts are the only way up from the plains; Cairne Bloodhoof founded and led it | [Thunder Bluff](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Thunder_Bluff) |
| Undercity: Trade Quarter at the centre, with the Magic Quarter, Rogues' Quarter, Apothecarium, War Quarter and Royal Quarter around it; ruled by Sylvanas Windrunner, the Banshee Queen | [Undercity](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Undercity) |
| *Forsaken Kingdom* covers the years between Arthas's return to Lordaeron and the rise of the Undercity, and introduces the Forsaken Paladin, whose story continues in Forever | [Blizzard: Forsaken Kingdom panel recap](https://news.blizzard.com/en-us/article/24302500/iii) |

Guard lines stay with places and attitudes. New combinations (orc mage, dwarf shaman, undead paladin, Skyborne) get opinions, never facts the sources don't give. Skyborne lines use only brief §10 (Windshapers through the Earthen Ring, Camp Gev'rek in Mulgore, druids few and elements before Elune, Highborne magister ancestry for the High Order).

## Lore sheet: Westfall (Westfall pack 1.0.0)

All original-WoW (Classic) facts; quest IDs come from capture only. Westfall is levels 10–20.

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Westfall was Stormwind's farmland; it went fallow after the Second War, farmers were driven off or left, and the Defias under Edwin VanCleef took over. Stormwind claims it but can't retake it; a few farmers hold on. Sentinel Hill (People's Militia, flight path), Moonbrook (Defias-held; the Deadmines entrance), Gold Coast Quarry, Westfall Lighthouse, Jangolode Mine (kobolds), Alexston Farmstead, Saldean's Farm, Furlbrow's Pumpkin Farm, the Molsen Farm, the Jansen Stead, the Dagger Hills, the Dust Plains. Hostiles: Defias, Riverpaw gnolls, harvest watchers and golems, kobolds, murlocs | [Westfall (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Westfall_(Classic)) |
| Gryan Stoutmantle: born in Westfall; trained as a Knight of the Silver Hand in Lordaeron; present at Stratholme (role unstated); left the Third War for the Defias at home; founded the People's Militia because Westfall's noble house was corrupted and Stormwind gave no help; dutiful, protective of farmers, pragmatic; turned down Magistrate Solomon; grudgingly grateful to Wiley the Black; with SI:7 learned VanCleef led the Brotherhood; a captured thief revealed the Deadmines | [Gryan Stoutmantle](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Gryan_Stoutmantle); [The People's Militia](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_People's_Militia) |
| Captain Danuvin: Sentinel Hill, People's Militia; rallies people to Gryan's campaign as a fight for dignity; Westfall won't forgive Stormwind's desertion. Patrolling Westfall: gnoll paws; the gnolls work with the Deadmines thieves | [Captain Danuvin](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Captain_Danuvin); [Patrolling Westfall](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Patrolling_Westfall) |
| Scout Galiaan: Red Leather Bandanas (fifteen); the Defias front keeps shifting; members can be tracked by the bandanas they wear | [Red Leather Bandanas](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Red_Leather_Bandanas) |
| Farmer Saldean: The Killing Fields, twenty harvest watchers; thieves drove the farmers out and harvest watchers took the fields. Their origin: Wiley's Note (Classic) says goblins built them for the Defias, to prey on the farmers' superstitions (Gryan says so; Galiaan suspects it; Saldean doesn't know) | [The Killing Fields](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Killing_Fields) |
| Salma Saldean: Saldean's Farm; wife of Farmer Saldean; friendly, welcoming, helps anyone who needs a meal and someone to listen; Westfall Stew; Goretusk Liver Pie (Classic). Westfall cooks once used murloc eyes and buzzard meat in lean times | [Salma Saldean](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Salma_Saldean); [Westfall Stew](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Westfall_Stew) |
| The Furlbrows (Theodore and Verna) ran a pumpkin farm in northern Westfall; the Defias overran it; their wagon broke on the road to Elwynn; they squat at the Jansen Stead with their horse Old Blanchy. Furlbrow's Deed: the Defias pressured him into giving it up; a note suggests using it as a template for forgeries. The Forgotten Heirloom: a pocket watch in a Defias house. Poor Old Blanchy: eight handfuls of oats; Blanchy is exhausted | [Farmer Furlbrow](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Farmer_Furlbrow); [Furlbrow's Deed](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Furlbrow's_Deed); [Poor Old Blanchy](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Poor_Old_Blanchy); [Westfall questing guide](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Westfall_questing_guide) |
| Captain Grayson: ghost of a pirate captain at Westfall Lighthouse; his ship wrecked when the lighthouse went dark; Old Murk-Eye's murlocs drove off, then killed, the keeper's family; he keeps the flame lit and means no harm. Quests: Keeper of the Flame, The Coast Isn't Clear, The Coastal Menace | [Captain Grayson](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Captain_Grayson) |

**Year 1 status (Forever):** the Defias hold Moonbrook; VanCleef is alive until a player clears the Deadmines (lines use the `dungeon:The Deadmines` deed, since every step of The Defias Brotherhood shares one title). **Not used (Cataclysm):** the Westfall Brigade, Vanessa VanCleef, the Furlbrows' murder, Hope Saldean, the Breadbasket Guild, Sentinel Hill's inn.

**Unsure / to confirm with capture:** whether Forever changed Westfall's quests; who gives Goretusk Liver Pie (the wiki lists both Salma and Verna); the names of Sentinel Hill's guard NPCs; Scout Galiaan's race; Quartermaster Lewis (left pooled until capture shows his quests).

## Stormwind: the Keep, the Cathedral, City Hall (Stormwind pack 1.0.0)

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Bolvar Fordragon: Highlord and Regent Lord, Supreme Commander for young King Anduin; Varian vanished en route to Theramore; Prestor urged Anduin's crowning; Bolvar respects her on dragons, disagreed about aiding Magistrate Solomon, won't move against her without proof; honorable, principled, compromise-seeking; secretly bound by a Drakefire Amulet fragment (never surfaced) | [Bolvar Fordragon](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Bolvar_Fordragon) |
| Katrana Prestor: high noble and royal adviser beside the boy king; secretly fuelled both nobles' and stonemasons' anger; dealt with the Defias to kidnap Varian; controlled Bolvar and nobles with the Drakefire Amulet; was Onyxia. Her secret never appears in dialogue; claim `prestor_patience` is exposed only by The Great Masquerade | [Katrana Prestor](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Katrana_Prestor) |
| Anduin Wrynn: ten when crowned; Bolvar regent; Prestor advises; worked to calm his people's fears | [Anduin Wrynn](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Anduin_Wrynn) |
| Baros Alexston: City Architect, City Hall in Cathedral Square; grew up on the Alexston Farmstead; a Stonemason who worked with VanCleef rebuilding the city; took the architect post instead of joining the rebellion; enemies since; The Unsent Letter | [Baros Alexston](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Baros_Alexston) |

**Not used:** Varian's return, the Onyxia reveal in Wrath, Anduin's priesthood, Baros's Draenor garrison and death.

## Zephras Isle (Zephras pack 1.0.0)

Only build prompt §10 (the Skyborne lore brief). No outside source used. Named NPCs other than High Elder Talaanis Shadowsong wait for capture data and the user's approval. The pools speak of customs and places the brief lists (Valanaar and the other towns, windstones, heirlooms, ashes to the wind, clothcraft, hippogryph-feather ornaments, the Peacekeepers and Empyrean Blades, the Al'Aketh's grey robes). Nothing on the speculation list, nothing on the Skyborne's views of the Legion, the Scourge or the Old Gods.

## Lore sheet: Redridge Mountains (Redridge pack 1.0.0)

Classic facts; levels 15–25. Quest IDs come from capture only.

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Redridge: Alliance region east of Elwynn; Lakeshire on Lake Everstill's north shore is the hub, constantly threatened by gnolls and Blackrock orcs; Three Corners links it to Elwynn, Duskwood and Stonewatch Keep; Blackrock orcs in Render's Valley and Stonewatch Keep, Shadowhide gnolls near the Tower of Ilgalar, Redridge gnolls, murlocs in the lake, dragon whelps on the highway; rumors of a lake monster | [Redridge Mountains (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Redridge_Mountains_(Classic)) |
| Lakeshire: Magistrate Solomon and the Court of Lakeshire govern; Marshal Marris and the Stormwind Guard keep it; a guard platoon held the town from Stonewatch Keep until orcs overran it; the Everstill Bridge was damaged and Foreman Oslow's repairs keep losing supplies to raids; Innkeeper Brianna's inn; residents include Verner Osgood, Chef Breanna, Martie Jainrose, Dockmaster Baren | [Lakeshire](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lakeshire) |
| Magistrate Solomon: elected; asked Stormwind's territories for reinforcements, none came; took Helendis Riverhorn's evidence of a Blackrock–black dragonflight alliance to Bolvar, who wouldn't send troops without proof and deputized adventurers instead; stern, impatient, little patience for idle talk. Classic quests: Messenger to Stormwind / Westfall / Darkshire, The True Masters, Wanted: Gath'Ilzogg, Wanted: Lieutenant Fangore | [Magistrate Solomon](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Magistrate_Solomon); [Messenger to Westfall](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Westfall) |
| Marshal Marris: by the bridge; worried about the orcs; wants Tharil'zun dead. Classic: Blackrock Menace, Shadow Magic, Tharil'zun, Missing In Action. (*Blackrock Blockade* is listed as a Forever quest; details unknown, not used) | [Marshal Marris](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Marshal_Marris) |
| Foreman Oslow: Everstill Bridge rebuild; tools sank with a boat hit by a catapult; gnolls stole building materials; businesslike, racing the rains, proud of on-schedule work. Classic: The Lost Tools, The Everstill Bridge | [Foreman Oslow](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Foreman_Oslow) |
| Verner Osgood: Lakeshire's blacksmith; his iron goes to the Guard so he can't shoe horses; gnolls take wandering horses; wants Yowler dead. The Price of Shoes sends Verner's Note to Smith Argus in Goldshire; then Return to Verner; also A Baying of Gnolls, Howling in the Hills, Underbelly Scales | [Verner Osgood](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Verner_Osgood); [The Price of Shoes](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Price_of_Shoes) |
| Corporal Keeshan: seized by Blackrock orcs during the battle of Stonewatch Keep and held at Render's Rock; Missing In Action is a level 25 escort | [Corporal Keeshan](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Corporal_Keeshan) |
| Wiley the Black: rogue at the Lakeshire Inn; owes Gryan Stoutmantle his life and repays him with information on the Defias; blunt; Gryan is frustrated by him | [Wiley the Black](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Wiley_the_Black) |
| Dockmaster Baren: Murloc Poachers, murlocs fishing the lake out; Selling Fish. Barkeep Daniels: Dry Times, a late liquor shipment, blames the orcs; wants Thunderbrew Lager (Grimbooze Thunderbrew, Westfall hills), Merlot (Stormwind), Moonshine (Darkshire), Sweet Rum (Goldshire) | [Murloc Poachers](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Murloc_Poachers); [Dry Times](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Dry_Times); [Redridge questing guide](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Redridge_Mountains_questing_guide) |

**Not used (later eras):** Cataclysm Lakeshire (Saving Foreman Oslow, Yowler Must Die!, Bravo Company, Keeshan's wife), Verner's death, Marris's drinking. **Kept out on purpose:** Princess Moira's capture (complicated canon). **Authored:** Keeshan overheard orcs grumbling about orders from "someone who isn't an orc" (consistent with The True Masters); Argus and Verner's old rivalry (built on their linked quests).

## Lore sheet: Duskwood (Duskwood pack 1.0.0)

Classic facts; levels 18–30. Quest IDs come from capture only.

| Fact | Source |
|---|---|
| Duskwood: the Scythe of Elune cursed what was southern Elwynn into a dark, dying forest of undead and worgen under black fog; ogres from Deadwind Pass; Darkshire the main town, Raven Hill nearby; Raven Hill Cemetery, Roland's Doom, the Yorgen Farmstead, the Rotting Orchard, Twilight Grove, Vul'Gol Ogre Mound; the Night Watch militia defends Darkshire; Morbent Fel is the dark figure in Forlorn Rowe | [Duskwood (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Duskwood_(Classic)) |
| Darkshire: largely abandoned by Stormwind's authorities; Stormwind pulled its troops out after Varian's disappearance; Lord Ello Ebonlocke mayor; Commander Althea Ebonlocke leads the Night Watch; Clerk Daltry keeps the (partly outdated) records; Tavernkeep Smitts runs the Scarlet Raven, where Jitters and Marshal Haggard have spent time; Madame Eva, Calor, the Carevins among residents | [Darkshire](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Darkshire) |
| Lord Ello Ebonlocke: mayor and head of the council; relative of Althea (unspecified); wants the Stormwind Army and asks where it is; Classic quests Note to the Mayor, The Embalmer's Revenge, Messenger to Darkshire, Translate Abercrombie's Note | [Lord Ello Ebonlocke](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lord_Ello_Ebonlocke) |
| Commander Althea Ebonlocke: led the Night Watch, sworn to protect Darkshire; Classic quests: The Night Watch chain, The Legend of Stalvan chain | [Commander Althea Ebonlocke](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Commander_Althea_Ebonlocke) |
| Tavernkeep Smitts: student of local lore who followed the Stalvan legend for years; investigated the visiting nobles' murders with Marshal Haggard; muddy journal pages he couldn't tie to Stalvan; saw Jitters writing in a book. Classic: Inquire at the Inn, Finding the Shadowy Figure, The Legend of Stalvan | [Tavernkeep Smitts](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Tavernkeep_Smitts) |
| Madame Eva: fortune-teller of the old arts (cards), ominous; claims the stars foretold the player. Classic: The Totem of Infliction, The Shadowy Figure, The Shadowy Search Continues, The Legend of Stalvan | [Madame Eva](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Madame_Eva) |
| Stalvan Mistmantle: studied under Crillian in Silverpine; headmaster at Moonbrook's schoolhouse; tutored the Flintridge family; obsessed with Tilloa Flintridge; when she saw him as an old uncle he murdered her and her intended, likely with an axe, possibly her family; retreated to Manor Mistmantle and became a mad undead. The Legend of Stalvan: thirteen parts, 28 to 35, ending at the manor and the Mistmantle Family Ring for Madame Eva | [Stalvan Mistmantle](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stalvan_Mistmantle); [The Legend of Stalvan (book)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Legend_of_Stalvan) |
| Jitters: ex-Defias in Raven Hill; jumpy and cowardly; escaped the worgen that killed his comrades after the Scythe of Elune was found at Roland's Doom; bought a blank book from Clerk Daltry and wrote at the Scarlet Raven; hid in the Yorgen barn and watched the Dark Riders kill Sven's family; buried his journal, which Sven saw. Classic: Raven Hill, Jitters' Growling Gut, Return to Jitters, Return to Sven, Finding the Shadowy Figure | [Jitters](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Jitters) |
| Sven Yorgen: camp at the Hushed Bank; remembers Duskwood before the dark; his wife and children killed by Dark Riders hunting the Scythe of Elune; bitter, blames Jitters. Classic chain: The Shadowy Figure, Return to Sven, Morbent Fel, Sven's Revenge and others | [Sven Yorgen](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Sven_Yorgen) |
| Abercrombie: grief-mad alchemist of Raven Hill; put his heart in his dead wife Eliza, who rose hungry for flesh and is kept buried; adventurers unknowingly help build Stitches (Supplies from Darkshire, Deliver the Thread, Zombie Juice, Ghoulish Effigy, Ogre Thieves, Note to the Mayor); he and Stitches attack Darkshire and are beaten; he survives | [Abercrombie](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Abercrombie) |

**Not used (later eras):** the Veiled Hand (Ello, Althea, the council), Smitts's death, Sven's worgen infection, Jitters working for Oliver Harris, Tobias Mistmantle's retail return. **Kept secret in dialogue:** Abercrombie's project (hinted, never stated, until Note to the Mayor). **Authored:** Eva's card names; Jitters' unpaid tab; Daltry's "thin" file. The Flintridges' summer cottage being Haggard's house (Haggard's page) is used carefully: Haggard only says the papers came with the house.

| Sara Timberlain makes fine linen from Elwynn Defias' red bandanas; Northshire's Defias wear burlap masks | [Red Linen Goods](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Red_Linen_Goods) |

## The Riverglades (Riverglades pack 1.0.0)

New in Forever (patch 1.60.1): a level 35-45 contested zone in southeastern Khaz Modan, east of the Badlands and Burning Steppes, reached by ship from Steamwheedle Port (Tanaris) to Powderfuse Port, or on foot from Redridge (Blackrock Pass, Redridge Pass), the Badlands and the Swamp of Sorrows. Nothing here is from training data: every fact below was read on warcraft.wiki.gg or Blizzard's own announcements on 2026-11-14. The wiki's Forever pages are new and partly stubs; anything they mark as speculation is left out.

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Zone: contested, 35-45; Alliance town Farholde Keep (Demar Brant), Horde town Rog'mar, ogre town Bolder'ok (Horde), neutral Powderfuse Port; subzones (Sunnyglade, Twilight's Shroud, Wheeler's Grange, Windstead, Ashwood's Fall, Bristle Hills, Eastwind Shore, Elbrim's Farm, Forlorn Pit, Meadowsbrook, Southern Watch, Terral's Watch, Turner's Logging Camp, Krol'dok Stronghold); oxbow lakes on the main river; boars, coyotes, crawdads, crocolisks, gnolls, harvest golems, murlocs, vultures | [Riverglades](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Riverglades) |
| History: Stormwind territory; the Old Horde sacked Sunnyglade in the First War, spared its mage tower to learn human magic, enslaved the survivors; families fled northeast and founded Windstead by a hidden lake; knights rescued the captives but were cut off, founded Meadowsbrook, then fortified a coastal island as Farholde; Stormwind judged the land lost and turned to the home front | [Riverglades](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Riverglades); [A Wartorn Land and The Brotherhood](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Wartorn_Land_and_The_Brotherhood) (in-game book, Farholde Keep) |
| Second War: Twilight's Hammer ogres and Stormreaver orcs fled east from the Tomb of Sargeras, joined an ogre mound already there; a Brotherhood of the Horse detachment was beaten back south and found Farholde and Meadowsbrook; Cho'gall's arrival "but a whisper" | same book, pages 3-4 |
| The Siege of Farholde: Rosalind Ashwood led Windstead's wagon families toward the northern watchtower, all killed there; Roderick the Unbroken held a bridge at Sunnyglade and lost an arm; Demar Brant ordered ditches and everyone inside the walls; siege, ships sabotaged from the water, rations thin; Sir Howard's dawn charge with Isolde Brightlance, Brienne Stormhart, Alden Amberhall and one-armed Roderick; Fern Crimsonleaf killed the Horde leaders in the night; the Horde fled to Krol'dok | same book, pages 5-16; [Siege of Farholde](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Siege_of_Farholde) |
| Memorials at Farholde (readable): Howard "the Stout", paladin, 2nd Division commander, fought to his last breath; Isolde, guidon-bearer, "died upright, banner held high" (tribute signed by Demar); Roderick, captain, "We never could find that blasted arm!" (signed Howard); Rosalind, lead scout, "eyes as keen as an elf"; Brienne, 1st Division stable master, "never was there a stallion she could not break"; Fern, lead intelligence agent, former pupil of Ravenholdt, "whereabouts unknown, presumed dead"; Alden, student of Antonidas, survived the charge, slain by a black dragon on an expedition west, tribute by his daughter Ashe, the expedition's sole survivor; and a Knights of the Brotherhood memorial | [Farholde Keep](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Farholde_Keep) and each name's page |

| Powderfuse Port: goblin town on the east coast, ship to Tanaris; Melbin Powderfuse, Master Goblin Engineer, says it was a vacant lot and he built and owns all of it; Innkeeper Zizplink ("fresh ocean air... a bit fishy"; Zizplink's Grocery List); Gray Dawnbright, a Brotherhood man drinking at the inn, rude, married to Tessa (Apple of My Eye); Jazzle Cheapshot's arena isn't running yet ("perfectly good coin" in fights the factions give away in the fields); Gritta Chumwater, fisherman, sells chum and fish; Fimbo Greasemitz's cafe on the water (boar and fish every day; A Taste of the Sea: murloc eyes, crab meat, a seafood stew); Argo Brinewhistle, drunk, The Monster Surfaces (bait from the murloc islands, a sea monster's head for his stern; reward Bottle of Powderfuse Port); Jezap Jinglesprocket, Gadgetzan fight promoter, out in the north (Bloodmaster's Ring) | [Powderfuse Port](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Powderfuse_Port) and each name's page; [A Taste of the Sea](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Taste_of_the_Sea); [The Monster Surfaces](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Monster_Surfaces) |
| Krol'dok Stronghold: ogre (Twilight's Hammer) dungeon, 40-45, north of Sunnyglade; bosses Slavecrusher Grox, Twilight Commander Zarmok, War Handler Ashka, Nar'gurk, Bossman Toof, Overlord Mugg | [Krol'dok Stronghold](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Krol%27dok_Stronghold) |
| Horde side (not written yet): Rog'mar (Krom'rosh gives Eye of Mugg; Neza Bloodsnout, hunter, Poison Tips; Tan'jani, troll headhunter, Fetch 'n' Fletch); Bolder'ok (Clugfist; Jom-jom's Escape) | quest pages above; [Jom-Jom's Escape](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Jom-Jom%27s_Escape) |
| Blizzard: "new but familiar" content; "a rugged frontier of rivers, grasslands, trade routes, struggling communities, and old grudges"; about the size of Stranglethorn, nearly 200 quests; gnoll camps, ogre threats, the Powderfuse goblins, the Twilight's Hammer and the Brotherhood of the Horse | [Found Photos panel recap](https://news.blizzard.com/en-us/article/24304071/world-of-warcraft-forever-found-photos-panel-recap); [Forever Now Live](https://worldofwarcraft.blizzard.com/en-us/news/24315820) |

**Wiki NPC IDs (not used; confirm with capture data, the pack keeps `TODO_NPC` names):** Demar Brant 257558, Ashe Amberhall 257591, Cady Lloydriel 269695, Sister Rowland 262031, Gretchen Mayberry 257087, Diseased Soldier 263611, Benjy Wheeler 258317, Gray Dawnbright 263120, Innkeeper Zizplink 255894, Melbin Powderfuse 260562, Jazzle Cheapshot 260568, Gritta Chumwater 260487, Jezap Jinglesprocket 257062. Quest IDs on the wiki: Eye of Mugg (Alliance) 94982, Lingering Essence 94997, Helping Hands 94861, A Taste of the Sea 95121, The Monster Surfaces 95267.

**Assumed (confirm in game):**
- The zone's name in game is "Riverglades" (the wiki title; text says "the Riverglades"). The pack loads on that name.
- Halligan Turner runs Turner's Logging Camp (the camp's name and his completion text; not stated outright).
- Sergeant Riftan is out at the front near Twilight's Shroud, not in the keep (his quest's coordinates are far west of Farholde).
- The Diseased Soldiers' illness has no stated cause, so nobody names one; Sister Rowland doesn't know either.
- Cady Lloydriel never says whose child is in the cradle. Nobody else does either.
- Gray Dawnbright's wife Tessa comes from a quest the wiki has no page for yet (Apple of My Eye): she's named, never described.

**Year 1 discipline:** Cho'gall stays a whisper (the book's own word). No Twilight Highlands, no Cataclysm, no Deathwing. The Twilight's Hammer here is cultists and ogres in Sunnyglade, Twilight's Shroud and Krol'dok; what they want stays half-told. Fern Crimsonleaf's fate is deliberately open (claim `fern_lives`). The black dragon that killed Alden is "a black dragon": no names.

**Authored, not canon:** the habits, worries and small talk of everyone below; Wheatley keeping Brienne's old tack; Gretchen's gryphons hating the river fog; Melbin's ledger of who owes him rent; Zizplink's grocery list as a running joke; Argo's toasts; Gray drinking because the Brotherhood sends him to watch the port (his posting at the port is the wiki's location; why he's there is authored and never stated as fact, he only grumbles).

## A human priest (core 0.29.0; Stormwind, Elwynn, World and the 30-45 zones)

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Shadowform (Classic): a priest talent; shadow damage up, physical damage taken down, and "you may not cast Holy spells while in this form". Trainers and healers ask a priest to step out of it before a lesson or near the sick for that reason | [Shadowform](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Shadowform) |
| Shape IDs: Shadowform reports as form 28; warrior stances (17-19) and rogue Stealth (30) also report as forms, so the addon treats those as no shape at all | [API GetShapeshiftFormID](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/API_GetShapeshiftFormID) |
| A bishop after the First War studied the orc necrolytes' magic to fight it, grew obsessed with why such dark energies existed, preached a balance of dark and light; after her death the Kirin Tor locked her writings away in Dalaran. Benedictus tells it without her name ("it ended badly for her"); how she died stays unsaid | [Shadow priest](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Shadow_priest); [Natalie Seline](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Natalie_Seline) (citing *World of Warcraft: The Magazine* Issue 4, p. 84) |
| Brother Nimetz, Rebel Camp's priest; Sister Rowland, Farholde's; Leonid Barthalomew at Light's Hope; Brother Anton, the Crusade's man at Nijel's Point; Stromgarde Badges (Refuge Pointe); the Hyal family and the hoofprints (Theramore) | entries above for each zone |

**Year 1 discipline:** the bishop's story is backstory from before Year 1, published later; she is never named and nothing after Year 1 is mentioned (no Cult of Forgotten Shadow, no Conclave, no Xal'atath). Benedictus knows of her; nobody else brings her up.

**Authored, not canon:** every NPC's reaction to Shadowform (Benedictus asking to see a priest's face, Laurena refusing to teach through it, Crowley's suspicion, Anduin's questions, guards' fright); priests' offers to bless riders, badges and graves; Jaina's prayer on the docks (a private memory, never an event); Smotts' cook having once asked him to say words for the crew.

## The Scarlet Path (throughline; World 1.17.0, Hillsbrad, Desolace, Stormwind, Eastern Plaguelands)

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Brother Crowley, Scarlet Crusade Emissary, a priest in the Cathedral of Light's lower level; sends Alliance adventurers to Brother Anton in Desolace (Brother Anton, level 39, requires 34) | [Brother Crowley](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Brother_Crowley); [Brother Anton (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Brother_Anton_(quest)) |
| Anton: the Scarlet Crusade has sworn to fight the Scourge body and soul; prove yourself on the undead ravagers of the Valley of Bones, to the south of Nijel's Point (coords 60,90) | [Down the Scarlet Path](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Down_the_Scarlet_Path) |
| Anton's Letter of Commendation to Raleigh the Devout in Southshore, who is to escort the recruit to the Scarlet Monastery; the true threat is in Lordaeron; Raleigh is alarmed that Anton still spreads the Crusade's word | [Down the Scarlet Path (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Down_the_Scarlet_Path_(2)) |
| Raleigh the Devout: human paladin, Southshore Inn upstairs; served the Crusade with pride, left after seeing at the Monastery in Tirisfal that everyone without the tabard was "plagued" to them and innocents were tortured; sends the player to kill High Inquisitor Whitemane, Scarlet Commander Mograine, Herod the Scarlet Champion and Houndmaster Loksey; afterwards: disciples of the Light don't revel in death, but some sacrifices are needed | [Raleigh the Devout](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Raleigh_the_Devout); [In the Name of the Light](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/In_the_Name_of_the_Light) |
| Scarlet Monastery wings (Classic): Houndmaster Loksey in the Library (Huntsman's Cloister), Herod in the Armory, Mograine and Whitemane in the Cathedral; Classic description: once a bastion of Lordaeron's priesthood, the Crusade sees all outsiders as potential plague carriers | [Scarlet Monastery](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Scarlet_Monastery); [Scarlet Monastery Library](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Scarlet_Monastery_Library); [Herod](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Herod) |
| Tyrosus left the Silver Hand when it became the Scarlet Crusade | Eastern Plaguelands section above |

**Wiki IDs (not used; the packs keep `TODO_` names):** Brother Crowley 12336, Raleigh the Devout 3980 (vanilla). Quests: Brother Anton 6141, Down the Scarlet Path 261 (Anton's), Down the Scarlet Path 1052 (the letter; **same title: capture data will report it ambiguous**, so set it by hand on Raleigh's `h_letter` and the thread's b3 condition), In the Name of the Light 1053.

**Year 1 discipline:** nothing from Raleigh's Burning Crusade appearance (Raleigh "the True" in Old Hillsbrad) or later (Southshore's ruin, Battle for Azeroth). The Monastery's leaders are named only as the quest names them; nothing about Whitemane and Mograine beyond that.

**Fixed while writing it:** Anton said the Valley of Bones was "to the southeast" (it's south); Benedictus said Crowley sends recruits "east" (Desolace is west, across the sea).

**Authored, not canon:** Crowley's warning about what the Crusade asks; Anton saying Raleigh writes less often; Raleigh watching the north road and not answering Anton's letters; the player's choice to tell Anton or spare him, and both of his reactions; Crowley calling Raleigh a deserter; Benedictus deciding to pray with everyone who goes down to Crowley. Raleigh's "a fortress with prayers painted on it" is his image, not a description of the building.

## The Mirage Raceway, deeper (Thousand Needles 1.2.0)

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Pozzik: vulture bones for light, strong frame tweaks (Load Lightening); Razzeric is the goblin team's eighteenth pilot, "a little off his rocker", who adds his own modifications (The Eighteenth Pilot); Gazlowe of Ratchet finds parts for the team, once worked on a rig, and owes them a prototype fuel regulator (Goblin Sponsorship); the gnomes got faster with identical designs, and Rizzle Brassbolts has been working on new plans (Keeping Pace); Rizzle's plans: indurium ore from Uldaman, heat-resistant (Rizzle's Schematics) | [Load Lightening](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Load_Lightening); [The Eighteenth Pilot](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Eighteenth_Pilot); [Goblin Sponsorship](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Goblin_Sponsorship); [Keeping Pace](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Keeping_Pace); [Rizzle's Schematics](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Rizzle%27s_Schematics); [Razzeric](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Razzeric) |
| Fizzle: salt-heavy scorpid venom for a very hot fuel (Salt Flat Venom); an engine fast enough to fly, but pistons crack; Martek the Exiled, a great smith in a Badlands camp with a goblin (Martek the Exiled); Martek: indurium resists heat, but true ore must come from Uldaman (News for Fizzle); the Brassbolts brothers (Fizzle, Wizzle with shells and tail fins, Rizzle with the plans) | [Salt Flat Venom](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Salt_Flat_Venom); [Martek the Exiled (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Martek_the_Exiled_(quest)); [News for Fizzle](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/News_for_Fizzle); [Rizzle Brassbolts](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Rizzle_Brassbolts); [Hardened Shells](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Hardened_Shells) |
| Martek the Exiled is an orc (Classic camp in the Valley of Fangs with goblins) | [Martek the Exiled](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Martek_the_Exiled) |
| Kravel: Delivery to the Gnomes (he removed a "small and worthless" piece from Fizzle's hydro ratchets and repeater bolts); Fool's Stout given to each pit boss (Get the Goblins Drunk / Get the Gnomes Drunk); Krazek in Booty Bay (The Rumormonger, Rumors for Kravel); Zanzil's Mixture via Crank Fizzlebub | [Delivery to the Gnomes](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Delivery_to_the_Gnomes); [Get the Goblins Drunk](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Get_the_Goblins_Drunk); [Kravel Koalbeard](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Kravel_Koalbeard) |

**Fixed:** Pozzik implied he still needed a pilot (Razzeric is the eighteenth already) and that the gnomes had no indurium (it's their edge); Fizzle said Martek knew about engines (he's a smith: metal).

**Authored, not canon:** Pozzik's suspicion of gnome spies and the gnome crew's pink ears; Fizzle's MEGA jokes and the brothers' "efficient arrangement"; Kravel's view of Ironforge's rules, and his answer to the Light.

## The Eastvale stables (Elwynn)

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Katie Hunter, Horse Breeder, and Randal Hunter, Riding Trainer, relatives (relation not stated), at the stables in the north of Eastvale Logging Camp; Katie sells horses to humans, and to others only when exalted with Stormwind, as her gossip explains (years of careful breeding; the horses mustn't fall into enemy hands) | [Katie Hunter](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Katie_Hunter); [Randal Hunter](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Randal_Hunter) |
| Classic riding: bought at level 40, one skill per race; another race's needed exalted reputation | [Riding](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Riding) ("Classic system") |

**Wiki IDs (not used):** Katie Hunter 384, Randal Hunter 4732. **Assumed:** Forever keeps riding at 40 (Classic). If it's lower, adjust the `minLevel = 40` gates in `npcs/eastvale_stables.lua`. An exalted non-human is treated like a human here (the `reputation` condition).


# Lore additions from the Redridge depth rewrite (for the lead to merge into LORE_NOTES.md, then delete)

## Redridge depth (pack 1.3.0)

Classic text only. Every page below was fetched raw from warcraft.wiki.gg and read; Cataclysm, Shadowlands and later sections were skipped.

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| The State of Lakeshire (Solomon's report, carried in Messenger to Stormwind): Stonewatch Keep lost; the Blackrock fought fuelled by Morganth's magic; Morganth then turned on the orcs, built an arcane tower in the northeast and raised the Shadowhide gnolls; a Blackrock offensive in the last fortnight brought the Everstill bridge down; supplies dwindled, no bandages for the wounded; he asks for soldiers, instruments of war, food and building supplies; he fears a darker evil brewing in the Northlands | [Reinforcing Redridge quest chain](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Reinforcing_Redridge_quest_chain); [Messenger to Stormwind](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Stormwind) |
| General Marcus Jonathan's reply: mourns the dead, will tell the King, hopes to bring reinforcements, adds in confidence a remark about His Majesty (Solomon never repeats it). Solomon on reading it: the correspondence is a mystery, something is amiss in the kingdom | [Reinforcing Redridge quest chain](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Reinforcing_Redridge_quest_chain); [Messenger to Stormwind (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Stormwind_(2)) |
| Gryan's reply: Solomon doesn't know Westfall's war; Stormwind deserted Westfall too. Solomon: what calls the army away from its people? Ebonlocke's reply: if he sent the Night Watch, Darkshire would fall; Solomon: the army left Westfall, the Guard left Darkshire, there is treachery somewhere | [Messenger to Westfall (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Westfall_(2)); [Messenger to Darkshire](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Darkshire); [Messenger to Darkshire (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Darkshire_(2)) |
| The True Masters: Helendis's letter ties the Blackrock to the black dragonflight; Solomon sends it to Bolvar; Lady Prestor calls him a "paranoid magistrate"; Bolvar deputizes the player; Solomon weeps on reading the decree; he sends the deputy to Marshal Maxwell | [The True Masters](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_True_Masters); [(2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_True_Masters_(2)); [(3)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_True_Masters_(3)); [(4)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_True_Masters_(4)); [(5)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_True_Masters_(5)) |
| Solomon is elected, judge of the Court of Lakeshire; Classic gossip: no time for idle talk while the orcs threaten the kingdom; Bailiff Conacher's gossip asks what business brings you before the Court and the Honorable Magistrate. The Town Hall page calls him kind and wise, now desperate | [Magistrate Solomon](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Magistrate_Solomon); [Court of Lakeshire](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Court_of_Lakeshire); [Lakeshire Town Hall](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lakeshire_Town_Hall) |
| Wanted: Gath'Ilzogg (Blackrock warlord, led the attack on Stonewatch, holed up in the keep; his death is "the first step in reclaiming the keep"); Wanted: Lieutenant Fangore (Shadowhide leader serving Morganth, "betrayer of Stormwind", bring his paw) | [Wanted: Gath'Ilzogg (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Wanted:_Gath%27Ilzogg_(quest)); [Wanted: Lieutenant Fangore (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Wanted:_Lieutenant_Fangore_(quest)); [Gath'Ilzogg](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Gath%27Ilzogg); [Stonewatch Keep](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stonewatch_Keep) |
| Kharan's Tale: months ago the Magistrate of Lakeshire sent envoys to Ironforge asking Magni for help against the Blackrock; Princess Moira volunteered and set out with twenty guardsmen; Dark Irons ambushed them in the Burning Steppes | [Kharan's Tale](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Kharan%27s_Tale) |
| Lakeshire in the First War: Blackhand's raiders took it; after the Second War the Alliance of Lordaeron rebuilt it, Turalyon overseeing the return of its people (Chronicle). No chapel or priest in the Classic town; dwarves and a night elf live there | [Lakeshire](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lakeshire) |
| Missing: Corporal Keeshan notice outside the inn: last seen at Stonewatch, dragged off by the Blackrock, believed held north of town west of the Burning Steppes pass; by Solomon's mandate, Marris rewards his rescuer | [Missing: Corporal Keeshan](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Missing:_Corporal_Keeshan) |
| Missing In Action is started by Keeshan in Render's Rock (his regiment overpowered, most killed, a blow to the head, captive, hurt badly) and ends at Marris; he reports as "Corporal Keeshan of the 12th Sabre Regiment", a mounted cavalry unit | [Missing In Action](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Missing_In_Action); [12th Sabre Regiment](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/12th_Sabre_Regiment); [Render's Rock](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Render%27s_Rock) |
| Blackrock Menace: the Blackrock came from the Burning Steppes and hold Stonewatch Keep, raiding north of town; more in the southeast pocket. Shadow Magic: shadowcasters' demon-tainted midnight orbs, to be destroyed. Tharil'zun leads the Outrunners ambushing between Lakeshire and Stonewatch; vicious and crafty | [Blackrock Menace](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Blackrock_Menace); [Shadow Magic](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Shadow_Magic); [Tharil'zun (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Tharil%27zun_(quest)) |
| Oslow's tools came by wagon from Goldshire, went into a boat when the bridge was blown out, sank when a catapult hit it (deepest point east of the bridge; the Lake Thresher patrols the deeps). The Everstill Bridge: iron smelted for weapons in the last invasion; a Stormwind shipment hijacked by Redridge gnolls into the hills; iron pikes and rivets. Classic gossip: on time and under budget | [Foreman Oslow](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Foreman_Oslow); [The Lost Tools](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Lost_Tools); [The Everstill Bridge](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Everstill_Bridge); [Lake Thresher](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lake_Thresher) |
| Speaking of Fortitude chain: Brother Paxton (Northshire's librarian, a priest) makes his own ink from Rethban ore, usually supplied by Oslow; Oslow is too busy fortifying and sends you to the Rethban Caverns north of town, named for Bart Rethban, where Redridge drudgers mine it | [Ink Supplies](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Ink_Supplies); [Rethban Ore (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Rethban_Ore_(quest)); [Brother Paxton](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Brother_Paxton); [Speaking of Fortitude](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Speaking_of_Fortitude) |
| Verner: Argus sends 50 horseshoes and a note asking to be paid; Underbelly Scales from black dragon whelps south of the lake pay the debt; brutes and mystics on the ridge north of his stable; Yowler's cry; the army too busy with orcs for gnolls | [Return to Verner](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Return_to_Verner); [Underbelly Scales](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Underbelly_Scales); [A Baying of Gnolls](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Baying_of_Gnolls); [Howling in the Hills](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Howling_in_the_Hills) |
| Wiley's Note to Gryan: the Defias control every mine from Westfall to Elwynn, use kobolds and gnolls, have goblins build metal monsters for the fields, tunnel from Booty Bay to Stormwind Keep, and build big (they were Stonemasons); Gryan wishes he'd let him rot; the note goes on to Shaw | [Wiley's Note](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Wiley%27s_Note); [The Defias Brotherhood (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Defias_Brotherhood_(2)) |
| Baren: Murloc Poachers completion wonders whether the murlocs are inland because they're running from something; Lake Everstill is famous for spotted sunfish; Classic gossip invites you to get a brew and talk | [Murloc Poachers](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Murloc_Poachers); [Selling Fish](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Selling_Fish); [Dockmaster Baren](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Dockmaster_Baren) |
| Daniels: Dry Times completion: his wife is a seamstress (she made the reward cloak) | [Dry Times](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Dry_Times) |
| Assessing the Threat: no men to spare, scout southern Redridge, then see Marris and Solomon. Parker's Classic gossip: Blackrock from Stonewatch, Shadowhide by the Tower of Ilgalar, the Redridge pack growing; Three Corners was once a lone guard's post | [Assessing the Threat](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Assessing_the_Threat); [Watch Captain Parker](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Watch_Captain_Parker); [Three Corners](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Three_Corners) |
| Visit the Herbalist: Parker asks you not to tell Martie who the flowers are from or for; Martie assumes you've found a sweetheart; daffodils are her favourite. Bellygrub forages southwest of town; no replanting until next season | [Visit the Herbalist](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Visit_the_Herbalist); [Delivering Daffodils](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Delivering_Daffodils); [An Unwelcome Guest](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/An_Unwelcome_Guest) |
| Redridge Goulash: Breanna fed the entire Stormwind brigade through the orc uprising and her cupboard is bare; condor meat, goretusk snout, spider meat; she teaches the recipe. Quote: hopes Stormwind's reinforcements arrive | [Redridge Goulash (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Redridge_Goulash_(quest)); [Chef Breanna](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Chef_Breanna) |

**Wiki IDs seen (not used; IDs come from capture only).** NPCs: Solomon 344, Marris 382, Oslow 341, Verner 415, Keeshan 349, Wiley 266, Baren 381, Daniels 346, Brianna 6727, Narnie 6728, Conacher 900, Feldon 1070, Parker 464, Darcy 379, Martie 342, Breanna 343, Morganth 397, Gath'Ilzogg 334, Tharil'zun 486, Fangore 703, Yowler 518, Lake Thresher 14357, Brother Paxton 951, Helendis Riverhorn 9562. Quests: Blackrock Menace 20, Tharil'zun 19, Shadow Magic 115, Dry Times 116, The Price of Shoes 118, Return to Verner 119, Messenger to Stormwind 120 / 121, Underbelly Scales 122, A Baying of Gnolls 124, The Lost Tools 125, Howling in the Hills 126, Selling Fish 127, A Free Lunch 129, Visit the Herbalist 130, Delivering Daffodils 131, The Defias Brotherhood 65 / 132, Messenger to Westfall 143 / 144, Messenger to Darkshire 145 / 146, Murloc Poachers 150, Wanted: Gath'Ilzogg 169, Wanted: Lieutenant Fangore 180, Missing In Action 219, Encroaching Gnolls 244, Assessing the Threat 246, Speaking of Fortitude 343, Brother Paxton 344, Ink Supplies 345, Rethban Ore 347, Solomon's Law 91, Redridge Goulash 92, The Everstill Bridge 89, The True Masters 4183-4186 and 4223, Kharan's Tale 4342. (An Unwelcome Guest shows 26509, the current version's ID; the Classic one may differ.) Objects: Missing: Corporal Keeshan notice 1726.

**Assumptions (confirm in game).**
- Quest titles used with TODO_QUEST: "Ink Supplies", "Rethban Ore", "Underbelly Scales", "A Baying of Gnolls", "Shadow Magic", "Selling Fish", "Kharan's Tale" (new this pass), as the wiki spells them.
- Keeshan respawns in Render's Rock after the escort; his after-rescue lines are written to make sense in the cave too ("Some nights I'm back on that rock").
- Solomon knows no dwarves arrived, not why; only a player who did Kharan's Tale can tell him about Moira.
- Brianna's wounded upstairs, and Solomon's "the guards will show you", are authored scenery: no wounded NPCs exist in Classic Lakeshire.
- The Forever quests listed on NPC pages (Marris: Blackrock Blockade; Feldon: Show of Force; Oslow: Alther's Mill; Solomon: WANTED: Incinerator Gar'im) have no wiki pages yet, so nothing from them is used.

**Year 1 notes (kept out).** Bravo Company and Colonel Troteman (Cataclysm naming; Classic Missing In Action names the 12th Sabre Regiment), Keeshan's wife Jade and his Cataclysm storyline, Watch Captain Parker, Darcy Parker and Libby, Oslow under the boulder, Yowler's sons, Grand Magus Doane and Darkblaze, Solomon's Orcish (a Cataclysm quest), the Scourge attack on Lakeshire (Shadowlands), Verner's death. The Traveler novels' Lakeshire (Greydon Thorne, the Glades) is post-Year 1 fiction and unused.

**Authored, not canon.** Solomon: the Cathedral sent prayers instead of a priest; the bandages ran out "a fortnight ago"; the "fortieth letter"; "next item"; lending Prestor a spear; "the court had something in its eye" (his weeping is canon, the joke isn't). Marris: the missing list; why he named Bellygrub; asking a dwarf how to beat the Blackrock. Oslow: the bridge plans, "ten years", Paxton's price joke. Verner: the warhorse joke. Keeshan: writing his comrades' names for Marris. Wiley: sightlines, the corner rent. Baren: hearing the catapult, the orcs counting boats. Daniels: his wife stitched half of Lakeshire's cloaks. Brianna: Narnie and the hearth. Conacher: the "polite drawer". Feldon: the Stormwind desk; asking a gnome how many gnolls make a war. Parker: "flowers say things". Martie: Parker and the weather. Breanna: no longer setting places by name.

(Two World rumors in rumors/redridge.lua were also corrected to match the quest text: the bridge shipment was iron pikes and rivets, and the Tower of Ilgalar is in the eastern hills.)


# Lore additions: Stormwind depth (for the lead to merge into LORE_NOTES.md, then delete)

## Stormwind depth (pack 1.6.0)

Scope: Duthorian Rall, Baros Alexston, Gazin Tenorm, Stephanie Turner, Lil Timmy, Mathias Shaw, Jennea Cannon, Gakin the Darkbinder, Innkeeper Allison, Lord Grayson Shadowbreaker, Arthur the Faithful, Grimand Elmore, Milton Sheaf, Elling Trias, Lord Gregor Lescovar, Dashel Stonefist, Furen Longbeard, Topper McNabb, Warden Thelwater, General Marcus Jonathan, Renzik "The Shiv", Major Samuelson, Thomas Miller. Every page below was read (raw wikitext) before writing; only Classic or Forever sections were used.

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Queen Tiffin was the strongest supporter of paying the Stonemasons; she was killed by a thrown rock while trying to calm the rioting masons (15 ADP, before Year 1). Used by Baros (grief) and Lescovar (as a weapon against the masons) | [Tiffin Ellerian Wrynn](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Tiffin_Ellerian_Wrynn) (citing *Exploring Azeroth: The Eastern Kingdoms* p. 10 and *World of Warcraft: The Comic*) |
| Senior Stonemasons were offered government posts instead of pay; VanCleef refused out of loyalty to the guild; Baros was offered the architect's post if he didn't join VanCleef; Thredd was captured in the riots and almost forgotten in the Stockade | [Bazil Thredd (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Bazil_Thredd_(quest)); [Report on the Defias Brotherhood](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Report_on_the_Defias_Brotherhood) |
| Baros grew up on the Alexston farm west of Sentinel Hill; the house was burned and taken by thieves; his father hid possessions there, among them his first compass (Humble Beginnings, Classic) | [Humble Beginnings](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Humble_Beginnings); [Baros Alexston](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Baros_Alexston) |
| The Lescovar Incident: Unsent Letter → Baros → Thelwater (Stockade Riots, Thredd's head) → the Curious Visitor "Maelik" → Shaw (Shadow of the Past; he owes Baros a favour; Maelik is Marzon, Lescovar's assassin) → Elling Trias (Look to an Old Friend) → Tyrion → The Attack! → Shaw (The Head of the Beast; Brotherhood's End: tell Alexston everything but who killed Lescovar, never mention Trias) → Baros → An Audience with the King (in Classic, Lady Prestor received the report because the King was away) | [Lescovar Incident quest chain](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lescovar_Incident_quest_chain); [Shadow of the Past](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Shadow_of_the_Past_(quest)); [Brotherhood's End](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Brotherhood%27s_End); [An Audience with the King](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/An_Audience_with_the_King); [The Curious Visitor](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Curious_Visitor) |
| Lescovar dismisses his guards for "meditation" in the Keep and calls Shaw a meddler | [The Attack!](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Attack!) |
| Shaw and VanCleef were childhood friends; Shaw trained him as a rogue hoping he'd join him; Shaw never questioned Prestor; he sent five agents to the Eastern Plaguelands and one returned babbling and died in his sleep | [Mathias Shaw](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mathias_Shaw); [Honor the Dead](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Honor_the_Dead_(quest)) |
| SI:7 rogue quests: Seek out SI: 7 / Snatch and Grab (Defias shipping schedule at Jerod's Landing, smuggling from Redridge to Westfall; SI:7 disavows caught agents); Mathias and the Defias / Klaven's Tower (Agent Kearnen hidden under a tree by the tower for weeks; Zanzil trolls' undeath poison; Venture Company); The Defias Brotherhood (3)/(4) (Wiley's note mentions Stonemasons; Shaw's report) | [Snatch and Grab](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Snatch_and_Grab); [Mathias and the Defias](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mathias_and_the_Defias); [Klaven's Tower](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Klaven%27s_Tower_(quest)); [The Defias Brotherhood (4)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Defias_Brotherhood_(4)) |
| Renzik was SI:7's spy in Horde lands, disliked people but liked the respect; Erion's Behest (night elf rogues sent from Darnassus); Kingly Shakedown (from Hulfdan Blackbeard, an old friend); Redridge Rendezvous (Lucius on Lakeshire's docks) | [Renzik "The Shiv"](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Renzik_%22The_Shiv%22); [Erion's Behest](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Erion%27s_Behest); [Redridge Rendezvous](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Redridge_Rendezvous) |
| The Missing Diplomat (levels 28-38): Thomas the altar boy → Bishop DeLavey (the Church's man at court, Petitioner's Chamber) → Jorgen (fisherman in the Valley of Heroes) → Elling → Watcher Backus on Darkshire's north road → Addle's Stead → Dashel Stonefist (Old Town; his thugs call him boss; his part ended when plan A failed; plan B was Slim from Menethil) → Mikhail → Theramore. Lines never reveal who the diplomat is | [The Missing Diplomat quest chain](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Missing_Diplomat_quest_chain); [The Missing Diplomat (8)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Missing_Diplomat_(8)); [(9)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Missing_Diplomat_(9)); [Bishop DeLavey](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Bishop_DeLavey); [Jorgen](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Jorgen) |
| Elling Trias's contacts reach from SI:7 to Hearthglen; Tyrion is an old friend; he sells Trias cheddar and Darnassian bleu | [Elling Trias](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Elling_Trias); [The Missing Diplomat (3)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Missing_Diplomat_(3)) |
| The Stockade: Thelwater was caught unprepared by the riots and kept them quiet ("if the Captain finds out"); Targorr the Dread (Blackrock executioner) and Dextren Ward (sold Raven Hill's dead to Morbent Fel; Millstipe wants his hand) were sentenced but held by the nobles; Mac, a 14-year-old guard, was stabbed for stopping prisoners tearing blankets into scarves (his grandmother Nikova Raskol, The Color of Blood); Thredd's visitor came once or twice a week with papers in order | [Warden Thelwater](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Warden_Thelwater); [Stormwind Stockade (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stormwind_Stockade_(Classic)); [Targorr the Dread](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Targorr_the_Dread); [Dextren Ward](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Dextren_Ward); [The Color of Blood](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Color_of_Blood) |
| Marcus Jonathan served under Turalyon with Reginald Windsor in the Second War; his letter to Solomon was written in confidence | [General Marcus Jonathan](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/General_Marcus_Jonathan); [Messenger to Stormwind (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Stormwind_(2)) |
| Forever: Bloodied Insignia (Ruins of Lordaeron, level 22): Lordaeron soldiers' insignia go to Marcus, who distributes them to next of kin | [Bloodied Insignia (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Bloodied_Insignia_(quest)) |
| The Valley of Heroes: five statues of the Alliance Expedition (Kurdran, Khadgar, Turalyon, Danath, Alleria), "presumed deceased"; Turalyon's plaque carries "By Blood and Honor We Serve"; Kurdran's epitaph is by Falstad Wildhammer | [Valley of Heroes](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Valley_of_Heroes) |
| Grayson: lord of the Silver Hand; the charger needs barding attuned to the spirit realm; Elmore made Grayson's own barding; Emphasis on Sacrifice (High Priest Rohan's censer, a donation feeding Ironforge's needy); Chillwind Point (Classic name) and Commander Ashlam Valorfist | [Lord Grayson Shadowbreaker](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lord_Grayson_Shadowbreaker); [The Work of Grimand Elmore](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Work_of_Grimand_Elmore); [Grimand's Finest Work](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Grimand%27s_Finest_Work); [Emphasis on Sacrifice](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Emphasis_on_Sacrifice); [Chillwind Camp (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Chillwind_Camp_(quest)) |
| Forever: A Friend of the Family (Captain Stoutfist → Bolvar) and (2) (Bolvar → Grayson): the Menethil Statuette; Terenas devout and humble, hated memorials, few survived; the Light has gathered in it | [A Friend of the Family](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Friend_of_the_Family); [A Friend of the Family (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Friend_of_the_Family_(2)) |
| Forever: The Shamed Lieutenant starts with Grayson and ends with Farren Orinelle (no quest page yet; only the title and the two NPCs are used) | [Lord Grayson Shadowbreaker](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lord_Grayson_Shadowbreaker); [Lieutenant Farren Orinelle](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lieutenant_Farren_Orinelle) |
| Arthur the Faithful starts The Tome of Nobility at 40 (to Rall; a warning against arrogance); Katherine the Pure trains beside him | [The Tome of Nobility (Stormwind City)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Tome_of_Nobility_(Stormwind_City)); [Katherine the Pure](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Katherine_the_Pure) |
| Rall: Gazin takes the Defias Script to the King's advisors after Rall sees it; Jordan Stilwell was to be a paladin before his father's craft; among the most faithful | [The Tome of Divinity (human 9)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Tome_of_Divinity_(human_9)); [The Test of Righteousness](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Test_of_Righteousness) |
| Gazin: wounds healed by the Cathedral's priests; Heroes' Vigil is an island in Stone Cairn Lake; they saw Defias going over documents, attacked, were overwhelmed by Defias mages; Henze held them off | [The Tome of Divinity (human 6)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Tome_of_Divinity_(human_6)); [(human 7)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Tome_of_Divinity_(human_7)); [(human 8)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Tome_of_Divinity_(human_8)) |
| Orphanage: just south-east of the Cathedral; orphans of the First and Second Wars and later the Scourge; young paladins donated clothes | [Orphanage (Stormwind City)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Orphanage_(Stormwind_City)) |
| Lil Timmy: "what does allergic mean?", two kittens (her sister), route along the canal through Cathedral Square, the Trade District, Old Town and the Dwarven District | [Lil Timmy](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Lil_Timmy) |
| Elmore: Elmore's Task / Stormpike's Delivery (an axe for Gringer Stormpike at Loch Modan's north tower); Grimand Elmore (warrior 28: outraged by Furen's notes, then intrigued); Blessed Arm (lightforge iron, a weapon against Morbent Fel, taken to Sven in Duskwood) | [Stormpike's Delivery](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stormpike%27s_Delivery); [Grimand Elmore (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Grimand_Elmore_(quest)); [Blessed Arm](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Blessed_Arm); [Armed and Ready (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Armed_and_Ready_(Classic)) |

**Assumptions (confirm in game):**
- Quest titles as the client shows them in Forever: "Seek out SI:7" (the wiki spells it "Seek out SI: 7"; existing line kept), "The Shamed Lieutenant" (no wiki page yet), "Grimand Elmore" (a warrior quest named after the smith), "Southshore" (Milton's quest).
- Several quests share one title across steps (The Missing Diplomat, The Defias Brotherhood, The Tome of Divinity, A Friend of the Family). Lines tied to them stay general enough for any step; Dashel's "no hard feelings" line now also needs his own "never met one" line heard first.
- Forever keeps the Classic Stockade, Lescovar chain and Missing Diplomat (the NPC pages carry Classic sections and Forever loot tables for Thredd, Targorr and Dextren Ward).

**Year 1 notes:** Varian is missing on the road to Theramore (Shaw, Samuelson) and the Missing Diplomat lines never say who the diplomat is; Prestor is trusted by Shaw and receives Baros's report (dramatic irony only); Windsor's fate is unknown to Marcus; nothing of Samuelson's later betrayal, Marcus's death, Renzik's Undermine past or death, Grayson's sister or Thelwater's Cataclysm-era Hogger story is used. Tiffin's death is pre-Year-1 backstory (15 ADP).

**Authored, not canon:**
- Shaw: a file on every noble house ("debts... to whom"); won't buy a priest's confessions; the creaking third step (existing).
- Grayson: counts the Hand each morning; thanks priests for patching his paladins; the Crusade kept the Hand's zeal and lost its mercy (opinion); "mercy to the ones who haven't earned it" is the hardest oath.
- Rall: keeps count of paladins who came back worse; the Light as a test, not a gift; priests start with mercy, paladins with courage.
- Marcus: greets the statues at night; keeps the insignia list in his own hand; has never seen Jorgen catch a fish; walks the wall at the watch change.
- Baros: thinks about his compass more than the Cathedral; sleeps better after Lescovar's death.
- Gazin: guilt about running ("which of us was the brave one").
- Stephanie: John's letters (strong beer, slow purses); her winter worry; asks a refugee what helped.
- Timmy: the dwarves smell of smoke; "two noes"; holy kittens.
- Jennea: worries about children dreaming in colours; enjoys arguing with priests; Tabetha is "brilliant, rude".
- Gakin: "ten in the dark rather than forty in the light"; the Church and the sword.
- Allison: Westfall folk eat first; the blessed bread; customers butter thinner.
- Arthur: prayed beside the masons; faith as trying to deserve a name.
- Elmore: swords for walls, real ones for guards; secretly used Furen's trick; why Stormwind needs good steel.
- Milton: dusts the Book of Medivh's empty shelf; shelves loudly near Lescovar.
- Elling: friends who like cheese; bishops who eat a great deal of it.
- Lescovar: taunts the Church over the masons' wages; wants the Deadmines papers "for the archive".
- Dashel: drinks slower near SI:7; "hit first or last, never in the middle".
- Furen: "we dinnae talk about once"; plans to hide behind the bellows; straps advice.
- Topper: "mine's got more orcs".
- Thelwater: the fishmonger's cart; "check the papers again".
- Renzik: likes being called sir.
- Samuelson: notices who forgets the order of bows.
- Thomas Miller: flour from Elwynn's mills; his margins.

(World additions from this pass: topics si7, stonemasons, the_stockade, valley_of_heroes; claim masons_killed_queen, partly true, open.)


## Elwynn depth, first half: Northshire, Goldshire's named NPCs, seven class trainers (pack 1.10.0)

NPCs: McBride, Willem, Eagan, Neals, Milly, Llane, Danil, Dughan, Farley, Remy, Argus, Melika, Wilhelm; trainers Khelden, Jorik, Drusilla, Lyria, Zaldimar, Keryn, Remen. All facts are Classic (original) versions; the Cataclysm Northshire (Blackrock invasion, burning vineyards, Sergeant Willem) and anything later is left out.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):**
- The Stolen Tome is in Forever (it was Classic until 3.3.0); the lines only fire on its quest state, plus one ungated Drusilla branch about the book.
- Forever's McBride quests A Scribbled Letter, The Big Picture and Follow That Kobold!, Dughan's Shhh! We're Hunting Kobolds, Book Return and Rare Books, and Remy's A Man About a Murloc, An Enticing Offer and Baited for Success have no wiki pages yet: not referenced.
- Remen Marcot has no trainer title on the wiki (Crowe is the warlock trainer); his file keeps `title = "Warlock Trainer"` and a "Train me." reply. Check in game and drop both if he only gives Gakin's Summons.
- Classic Farley's wares (the wiki lists retail cheeses; the Classic list may differ).
- Quest title "Seek out SI: 7" (with a space): Keryn's lines now use it. `RPGDialogue_Stormwind/npcs/city.lua` still says `"Seek out SI:7"`, which can't match after punctuation is dropped. Fix there.

**Year 1 notes:** Farley's brother Frandis is pre-Year-1 backstory published later (*Before the Storm*, *Tides of War*). Farley says only what he'd know in Year 1: his brother's caravan went to Lordaeron in the plague year and never came back. Nothing about the Forsaken, Theramore, Anduin's summons or the Arcantina. Dughan's *Stormrage* dream and the *Dark Riders* comic aren't used. Medivh's coma at Northshire is pre-Year-1 history Neals tells from the library.

**Authored, not canon:**
- McBride: asked Stormwind twice for soldiers (bible secret), his view of Dughan, the novice "carrying candles last spring", reading Garrick's stance as drilling.
- Willem: "a shirt and a stick"; fearing to know faces under the bandanas.
- Eagan: wolves keep clear of the kobold mine; cooking over coals; Helene the better skinner.
- Neals: thanking Faol nightly; locking the cellar twice; the novice who read the tallies.
- Milly: lying awake over the guards' boots; vines growing back from the root.
- Llane: "before that I was one"; the priest's lesson.
- Danil: no coin under the Abbey roof; stale loaves for novices; Neals calling trade a prayer.
- Dughan: guessing "someone who was owed money" trains the Defias; the desk he sees more of than his bed; words for the footmen at Thomas's bridge.
- Farley: Verina keeps the books and lights a candle each winter; "my father and his before him"; slept in the messenger's room once; asking a Lordaeron refugee whether it was quick.
- Remy: evasive about Redridge; dwarves and gold.
- Argus: deaf in one ear from the anvil (built on "SPEAK UP!"); the apprentice and the quench.
- Melika: Dobbins' scalded hand; tasting Tomas's stew.
- Wilhelm: sharing the yard with Lyria and the noon quarrel; Josetta sitting with the dying.
- Trainers: Khelden would rather the Defias had the Void book than La Salle; Jorik's quiet horses; Drusilla has read three chapters; Zaldimar reads through the singing; Keryn's dreadful romance; Remen's "law of nature".

### World pack additions
None needed: every topic, claim and thread used already exists (`defias`, `kobolds`, `echo_ridge`, `hogger`, `murlocs_east`, `the_collector`, `gold_dust`, `northshire_abbey`, `lions_pride`, `westfall`, `people_militia`, `lost_footmen`, `eastvale`, `morganth`, `stalvan`, `tome_of_divinity`; claims `defias_trained`, `defias_common_thieves`, `murlocs_in_elwynn`, `remy_only_buyer`, `hogger_unbeatable`).


## Elwynn depth, second half: farms, Eastvale, Goldshire's townsfolk (pack: next minor after 1.9.0, set by the lead)

Scope: falkhaan, maybell, tommyjoe, gramma, thomas, haggard, raelen, theocritus, rainer, bernice, ma_stonefield, billy, sammuel, sara, gerard, sam, henze, pestle, and the fourteen Goldshire folk. All quest facts are the Classic (original) versions; Cataclysm rewrites are listed under "Not used".
