**Wiki IDs seen (not used; IDs come from capture only):** Falkhaan Isenstrider NPC 6774; quests A Visit With Maybell 26150 (Cataclysm), Speak with Gramma 111, Collecting Kelp 112, The Escape 114, Kobold Candles 60, Shipment to Stormwind 61, Further Concerns 35, Find the Lost Guards 37, Deliver Thomas' Report 39, Discover Rolf's Fate 45, Protect the Frontier 52, Report to Thomas 71, Report to Gryan Stoutmantle 109, Marshal Haggard 1666, Dead-tooth Jack 1667, The Legend of Stalvan 74 / 75 / 78, A Bundle of Trouble 5545, Theocritus' Retrieval 178, A Watchful Eye 94, Looking Further 248, Morganth 249, Riverpaw Gnoll Bounty 11, Westbrook Garrison Needs Help! 239, Lost Necklace 85, Back to Billy 84, Pie for Billy 86, Goldtooth 87, Princess Must Die! 330 in the questbox but 88 in the external link (ambiguous), Red Linen Goods 83, Give Gerard a Drink 16, The Symbol of Life (human) 1790, The Adventurer 96627, The Great Outdoors 96607, Camping 101: First Aid 96047, Camping 101: Cooking 96658, Rough Wolf Pelts 91751.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):**
- Forever uses the Classic versions of these quests: Princess at the Brackwell patch with her entourage, Goldtooth inside the Fargodeep Mine, Dead-tooth Jack as a warrior quest, the Theocritus chain, Deliver Thomas' Report, Shipment to Stormwind.
- `questDone "The Symbol of Life"` stands for "Henze was revived", as in the Stormwind pack (Gazin, Rall). The quest actually turned in at Henze is "The Tome of Divinity" (human 7), whose title several steps share.
- `TODO_QUEST("Dead-Tooth Jack")` (here and in World towns.lua) differs in case from the wiki's "Dead-tooth Jack"; titles are matched case-insensitively, so it's left alone.
- Erma, as a Year 1 stable master, keeps hunters' beasts; Michelle Belle teaches First Aid (her file title stays "Physician").
- The tent is the leatherworking camp fixture (The Great Outdoors says so; the Camping 101: Leatherworking page doesn't exist yet).
- Jason Mathers stands on Lee Brown's pier in Forever (Lee calls him "Jason down the end").
- Ma is used as Ma Stonefield's name only; whose mother she is stays unsaid.

**Year 1 notes / not used:** A Visit With Maybell (Cataclysm); the Maclure-Stonefield marriage and the Lunarfall barn (Draenor); Hero's Call: Westfall!, Marshal Patterson, Goss the Swift, James Clark, Bounty on Murlocs, Cloth and Leather Armor (Cataclysm); Sara's Stormwind Army commission (from a Cataclysm quest); Theocritus's Kirin Tor past and Karlain (the Dark Riders comic), the Gadgetzan Times interview, Cote Metcalf (Legion); Sammuel's Legion, Dragonflight and Midnight roles and Season of Discovery's Relics of the Light; the Powers of the Void "stolen tome"; the Lands of Conflict RPG's Westbrook and Azora material. Where Stormwind's army is "abroad" is never stated.

**Removed as invented life stories (ids kept, text replaced):** Sammuel's farm-boy-from-Eastvale origin (p_cart, a_never); Michelle Belle's father in the Second War (w_father); Helene's mother and grandmother (l_mother); Krighton's apprenticeship during the rebuilding (t_rebuild); Raelen's career before the camp (never shipped). **Corrected to canon:** Bernice no longer apologises to Billy (he did take it); Ma Stonefield's Brackwells are gone, not haughty; Helene names Eagan as her husband; Tharynn's "corner of an inn" is now his wagon; Haggard took Jack for Dughan; Pestle's Escape line no longer says the lovers "got away".

**Authored, not canon:** Falkhaan's "rest comes to anyone under that roof", the Westfall folk passing him asking for work, the novices' shoulders; Maybell meeting Tommy Joe "across a fence", the peacebloom patch, her refusal to learn stealth; Tommy Joe watching the vineyard roofs from the bend; Gramma's unnamed unforgiven, "betting on the young ones"; Thomas's regret at knowing only his men's names and rota; Haggard's priests who eased the ache but not the fog, the loggers' bear behind the woodpile; Raelen's worry about uncomplaining Westfall workers and the map for the army; Theocritus never having asked where his gnomes come from, "the seal is the most important thing in it"; Rainer's "abroad, and nobody tells a deputy where"; Bernice sleeping in the necklace; Billy checking the river for grapes twice; Sara hearing the camp's news from her step; Gerard's "I don't have feuds, I have blisters"; Sam's fireside guests; Henze's joke from home; Pestle's peacebloom-before-noon superstition; the Goldshire folk's trade talk (Toddrick's sausages, Tharynn's green door, Krighton's lion and oak, the Steeles' leather argument, Erma's grey waiting at the road, Crowe's "strength first, then pacts", Lee's pier at dawn, Adele's boots).

**For the lead (not my files):** the World thread `stalvan.b2_haggard` and Duskwood's `smitts.h_haggard` are flagged choppy by the new voice checker; `heardLine` targets added this pass: `billy.m_river`, `billy.s_slip`, `adele.hb_default` (all `key = true`), and `rainer.h_raelen` is now `key = true`.


## Duskwood depth (pack 1.3.0)

| The Night Watch was raised and trained by the forest's own people; its foes are the undead of Raven Hill and Tranquil Gardens and the worgen of Brightwood and Roland's Doom | [Night Watch](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Night_Watch); [Messenger to Darkshire (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Darkshire_(2)) |
| Ello refuses Solomon: the Watch exists because Stormwind left, and sending it would let the town be overrun before it reached the forest's edge | [Messenger to Darkshire (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Messenger_to_Darkshire_(2)) |
| Ello has never heard of Abercrombie and can't read the ancient script of his note; Sirra reads old languages; the translation turns Ello white; Stitches then walks the road from Raven Hill and kills the Watch at two camps | [Note to the Mayor (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Note_to_the_Mayor_(Classic)); [Translate Abercrombie's Note](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Translate_Abercrombie%27s_Note); [Translation to Ello](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Translation_to_Ello); [Stitches](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stitches) |

### Assumptions (confirm in game)
- Quest titles as matched by `TODO_QUEST`: "Proving Your Worth", "Armed and Ready", "Seeking Wisdom", "Raven Hill", "Ogre Thieves", "Ghost Hair Thread", "Return the Comb", "Gather Rot Blossoms", "Juice Delivery", "Translation to Ello", "Bride of the Embalmer", "Morgan Ladimore", "Mor'Ladim" (the wiki's quest box spells it "Mor'ladim"; titles are normalised, so case shouldn't matter).
- Multi-part chains share a title (The Legend of Stalvan, The Night Watch, Worgen in the Woods, Look To The Stars, Messenger to Darkshire). Lines on them are written to fit any part; nothing says Stalvan is dead.
- That Forever keeps the Classic Embalmer chain (Translation to Ello sets Stitches walking; Bride of the Embalmer follows). If Forever uses The Embalmer's Revenge instead, swap the conditions back.

### Year 1 notes
- **The Scythe of Elune is Classic content in Duskwood.** The wiki is clear: Jitters' Completed Journal names it, Sven's Classic chain turns on it, and The Scythe of Elune (Classic) chain (Caravaneer Ruzzgot, Daltry, Jonathan Carevin, Thyn'tel Bladeweaver in Darnassus; removed in 4.0.3a) is about it. What's post-Classic is its night elf history (Goldrinn, Druids of the Pack, *Curse of the Worgen*, the *Dark Riders* comic, Gilneas). Following the brief, nothing new names the Scythe or links the worgen to night elves; Jitters says "rune-carved wood" and "the thing". Existing lines that mention "a scythe" (Sven f_default), "something found in Roland's Doom" (Eva tp_worgen) and Jonathan's Carevin Family line about "a night elf priestess" were left as they were: they're accurate to Classic.
- **The Embalmer's Revenge is the Cataclysm quest**; Classic has Translation to Ello (Stitches walks) and Bride of the Embalmer. Ello, Abercrombie and the Night Watch pool (`pools.lua` l_stitches, fixed during the voice pass) now use those. Still on the old title, in the World pack: `RPGDialogue_World/rumors/duskwood.lua` (stitches) and `RPGDialogue_World/towns.lua` (darkshire quests). Replacements below.
- Bishop Farthing appears only as Year 1's kind bishop of the Cathedral (Seeking Wisdom). Nothing later about him is hinted.
- Natalie Seline is never mentioned. Raven Hill's dead are Morgan Ladimore's family, Eliza, and the town's.
- Lady Prestor's part in the withdrawal is never stated: Millstipe says only "someone at court".
- Morgan Ladimore's surviving daughter (The Daughter Who Lived) stays out, as before.
- Not used: the Veiled Hand and every Legion death or betrayal; Sven's worgen infection; Oliver Harris; Tobias Mistmantle; the Dark Riders comic; Beggar's Haunt.

### Authored, not canon
Ello's eleven letters and the framed reply; the council not discussing worgen after dark; Althea's watchers' trades and their pay "in lamp oil and gratitude"; Smitts' stopping brewing zombie juice, Jitters' unpaid tab, nobody asking Daltry about the handwriting at the time; every one of Eva's card names, that Alyssa isn't told, that Eva chose not to ask what the thread was for; Daltry's form for the walking dead and his short book of people the Watch likes; Jitters' lamp in the window and keeping his eyes open while someone prays; Sven sitting on his porch after Morbent Fel, "the Light can have the nights", reading Jitters' prayer twice; Abercrombie's unease at a paladin's shine and his opinion of Morbent's "crude work"; Jonathan's list of heretics; Calor's one-line prayer and drinking with watchers; Sirra's old liturgies on Raven Hill stones and his laundry-list test; Viktori's three new stars; Grual's stew and the food sent up to Jitters; Millstipe's "fifty soldiers and a priest"; Trelayne's warning about voices calling names; Clarise's work for the Watch.

**Corrections to LORE_NOTES.md (Westfall lore sheet):**
- "Their origin: Wiley's Note (Classic) says goblins built them for the Defias, to prey on the farmers' superstitions (Gryan says so; Galiaan suspects it; Saldean doesn't know)" is superseded by Wiley's Note (Classic item): goblins built them for the Defias to frighten superstitious farmers. Saldean still doesn't know; Gryan learns it from the note (reply gated on hearing Wiley's `h_defias`); Galiaan guesses it.
- Gryan: "Westfall's noble house was corrupted" should read "Stormwind's House of Nobles (corrupted by Prestor) gave Westfall no support". Gryan's `s_default` now says "The House of Nobles went rotten".
- Goretusk Liver Pie: the quest page gives Salma as giver (the questing guide lists Verna). Lines treat it as Salma's.
- Theodore's pocket watch was Verna's father's wedding gift (the 1.0 line said his own father's; fixed).
- Quartermaster Lewis now has his quests (A Swift Message, Return to Lewis) in his file.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):**
- Every step of The People's Militia, The Defias Brotherhood and Messenger to Westfall shares one title, and a TODO title resolves to one ID, so `questActive` on those titles may track only one step. Lines that use them say nothing step-specific ("you're on the trail already", "about Solomon's letter").
- Thor's "Continue to Stormwind" reply assumes he still offers that step in Forever.
- Lewis's ammunition reply assumes Forever kept Classic's arrows, shot and throwing weapons on him.

**Year 1 notes:** Bolvar is Regent ("someone keeps telling the Regent Westfall can wait": Prestor is never named). Queen Tiffin's death in the riots is later-published backstory and is not used. The Juggernaut is not mentioned by name; Gryan and Wiley only talk of "what they're building under Moonbrook". Nothing about the Brigade, Northrend, Vanessa or the homeless.

**Authored, not canon:**
- Gryan: no chaplain on Sentinel Hill; he says words over the graves; the militia buries the Defias dead too; militia asking a priest for blessings before patrols; his reply to Wiley's "not about farms"; the oath's wording; the holdouts' tithe being habit, pride and hope.
- Danuvin: teaching farmers to stop and tie up a thief because Gryan wants prisoners; his wish never to hold a spear again.
- Galiaan: his scouting rules; his unproven guess about the watchers; smugglers running dark past the lighthouse.
- Saldean: he knows Salma feeds Defias; seed put by for a neighbour.
- Salma: she adds salt to Verna's recipe; a Defias boy she fed who then left her hens alone; prays at the stove; a grace over the pot.
- Theodore: the wheel split through and nobody to mend it; he begged Saldean to come.
- Verna: Theodore got her out before his boots were on; she disagrees with Salma about the city.
- Grayson: the ship's many names; his guess at Murk-Eye's motive; he'll accept rest only when the coast is safe.
- Daphne: orchard prayers; fear of fire; men with crates and goblins seen at night.
- Heather's slate for the militia; Lewis's triplicate forms and the bread bin; Thor's lost arrow crate and nameless gryphons; Riell's rarely used bed at the inn.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):**
- Karl Boran's quests: the wiki merges Classic and Cataclysm versions (the pages read describe the Cataclysm flood and Gobbler). The Blind Princess and the statuette are taken as Classic (item 2625 is a Classic item ID); Claws from the Deep stays general.
- The Theramore boat is not named (the wiki's "Lady Mehley" may be the Classic name; not used).
- `visited = "Ironforge"` (Flagongut's notice, unchanged) depends on what subzone the client reports inside Ironforge.
- Forever's Stoutfist quests without wiki pages (Forced Disarmament, From the Ashes, Old Habits, This Land Was Their Land) are not referenced.

**Year 1 notes:** No Cataclysm (flood, crabs, Gobbler, Fitzsimmons dead, Horghast, the Twilight's Hammer in Dun Modr, Thargas Anvilmar's victory). No Night of the Dragon (raptor attacks from Grim Batol). Derek Proudmoore and the Battle of Crestfall left out (later sources). Mikhail never names the king: the Missing Diplomat parts share one title, so the engine can't tell the reveal from the start. Aman left out for the same reason.

**Corrections to earlier content (ids kept):** Stoutfist's `h_banners` and Flagongut's `h_default`, `h_stoutfist`, `d_default`, `tp_dragonmaw` said the Dragonmaw camped on Whelgar's dig; the sources put them at Angerfang in the hills, with raptors on the dig.

**Voice pass (2026-11-15):** every line in the pack, the guard pool included, was rewritten as whole sentences (wording only: same facts, ids, conditions). The topic takes below are written in the new voice too.

**Authored, not canon:** see "Depth rewrite (Wetlands 1.2.0)" in `bibles/menethil.md` (Stoutfist's night watch and burial ground, Fitzsimmons's dreams and mutiny joke, Glorin hiding the mead and asking where a priest trained, Harlo counting Dark Irons, Karl's lantern count, Helbrek's good mugs, Flagongut's two ales, Mikhail's full mug).

**Corrections made in the pack:** Jordan's lines keyed on a quest titled "Verigan's Fist", which is the item; the quests are The Test of Righteousness. Belm's arrival line keyed on Bound for Kharanos, a Cataclysm quest; it now keys on Senir's Observations (the Coldridge report carried through Kharanos). Sten's mail line named Grelin as the addressee; the Classic quest sends the letters to Talin Keeneye first.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):** Forever keeps the Classic quest titles used here (The Test of Righteousness, Senir's Observations, The Reports, The Lost Dwarves, Letter to Stormpike, Mountaineer Stormpike's Task, A Visitor to Dun Morogh, Farsen's Watch, Old Ironforge Incursion, Prehistoric Prism). Forever's Senir Whitebeard also gives "Secure the Mountain" (no wiki page yet), not referenced. Forever lets dwarves be shamans (Teo Hammerstorm, Archaic Rune); a dwarf shaman gets a rune line from Sten. Whether Forever allows gnome priests is unconfirmed (Hallowed Memorandum suggests it); no line assumes it.

**Year 1 notes:** Magni is alive and king; Moira stays a whisper ("in the hands of the Dark Iron"; a level-50 line says others have gone into the mountain for her). The Classic Moira quests are Kharan Mighthammer's chain and The Princess's Surprise (Blackrock Depths); "The Princess Trapped" is an Arathi Highlands quest about Myzrael and isn't used. Brann is missing, Muradin believed dead. Mekkatorque plans a return but there is no Operation: Gnomeregan. Grimbooze is Ragnar's brother (Classic), not his father (Battle for Azeroth). The Ashbringer, Eimear and Mekkatorque's comic details are later-published backstory of events before Year 1; used lightly and never named where it would add a fact beyond what's cited.

**Authored, not canon:** Magni's eyes-shut tram rides and the Senate's seasonal reminders; his hint that the Frostmane cost his house dear; Mekkatorque's spare parts in the trophy case; Archesonus's wedding joke and her reading of the plague; Magellas keeping Brann's chair out of tidiness; Stormpike's "every cipher is written by somebody lazy"; Stonehallow's opinion that dwarven digging wakes the troggs; Firebrew's gryphon-wrestling greybeard and John Turner's coin-from-shame; Brandur's "same oaths, better beer"; John's "the months are very close together"; Jordan liking to watch the road; Eric's bean-eating hunter; Sten's wolves without a vote; Grelin's beard; Senir's warm posting; Ragnar's self-tapping keg; Belm pretending not to see the rogue in his kitchen.


## Loch Modan depth (Loch Modan pack 1.2.0)

All facts are original-game (Classic). Wiki pages read 2026-11-14.

| Mountaineer Stormpike in the northern tower (Algaz Station) wants a runner and boasts of his exploits; Pilot Stonegear at Steelgrill's Depot seeks a rare ore; Mountaineer Rockgar at Dun Algaz leads a mission into the Wetlands | [Mountaineer Stormpike's Task](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mountaineer_Stormpike%27s_Task); [Mountaineer Stormpike](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mountaineer_Stormpike); [Stonegear's Search](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stonegear%27s_Search); [Report to Mountaineer Rockgar](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Report_to_Mountaineer_Rockgar) |

**Wiki IDs seen (not used):** quests Rat Catching 416, Find Agmond 738, Ironband's Excavation 436.

**Corrections made in the pack:** Kadrell's Rat Catching line now describes kobolds in the western foothills, not cellar rats; the Mountaineers' pool listed gnolls among the loch's threats (gnolls are Cataclysm-era Loch Modan) and now lists kobolds.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):** Forever keeps the Classic quest titles used (The Trogg Threat, In Defense of the King's Lands, A Dark Threat Looms, Protecting the Shipment, Agmond's Fate, Ironband Wants You!, Report to Mountaineer Rockgar). Captain Rugelfuss has a Forever turn-in, "Banner of the Fallen (quest)", with no wiki page yet; not referenced.

**Year 1 notes:** the dam stands; Hinderweir is on it; no Twilight's Hammer at Mo'grosh, no Brann in Thelsamar.

**Authored, not canon:** Kadrell's dry socks for the Wetlands and the soot under a spy's nails; Rugelfuss digging trenches and "more chieftains than sense"; Hinderweir checking at noon and "who keeps the last stone"; the miners' song about the dam; Hearthstove's broken stool; Vidra straining the ichor and "bless the bears before".

**Not used (and why):** "Tyrande teaches that the Holy Light and the Light of Elune are closely related" and "the Church sent members to the Sisterhood to lead them to the Light" both come from Season of Discovery quests (Meditation on Elune, Meditation on the Light (human)). So no NPC states how the two faiths relate; Tyrande and A'moora say what they've seen and leave it open. Crown of the Earth (7)/(8) (Forever, Fandral to Tyrande) and Exploring the Alliance (Forever) have no wiki pages yet, so they aren't referenced. Doling Justice (2) shares its title with part 1, so it isn't used as a condition.

**Wiki IDs seen (not used; the pack keeps TODO_ names):** NPCs: Mathrengyl Bearwalker 4217, Priestess A'moora 7313, Gracina Spiritmight 7740. Quests: Crown of the Earth (6) 935, Teldrassil 940, Grove of the Ancients 952, Reception from Tyrande 1081, Heeding the Call (Alliance) 5923, Back to Darnassus 5931, Body and Heart (Alliance) 6001, Lessons Anew (Alliance) 6121, Power over Poison (Alliance) 6125, A Lesson to Learn (Alliance) 27, Aquatic Form (Alliance) 5061, To Darnassus (Forever) 98397, The Temple of the Moon (Classic) 2519, Tears of the Moon (Classic) 2518, Sathrah's Sacrifice 2520, Rise of the Silithid (Alliance) 162, Rise of the Silithid (Feralas) 4267, Desperate Prayer (Darnassus) 5640.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):** that Forever's quest is titled exactly "The Great Cat Spirit"; that Alathea is still the Temple's priest trainer in Forever; that "Teldrassil" as a quest title resolves (the zone shares the name).

**Year 1 notes:** no Valstann, no morrowgrain, no Nightmare, nothing that hints at Fandral's fall (his pride and his burden only). No wedding: Tyrande says "Malfurion".

**Authored, not canon:** see the "Depth pass (Darnassus 1.2.0)" section of `bibles/darnassus.md`.

## World-pack additions (ready to paste into RPGDialogue_World/topics.lua)

NPCs in Darnassus, Teldrassil, Darkshore and Ashenvale already answer these topics in their `topics` tables; they only become askable once the topics exist. `learn` effects for them were left out so the validator stays quiet.



Who answers what: teldrassil (Tyrande, Fandral `next=tree`, Tenaron, Corithras), malfurion (Tyrande `next=malfurion`, Fandral), sisterhood_of_elune (Tyrande, A'moora, Gwennyth), sentinels (Tyrande), warsong (Tyrande `next=ashenvale`, Raene, Senani, Mastok), silithid (Gracina `next=silithid`), fel_moss (Melithar, Athridas, Terenthis `next=moss`, Thundris), furbolgs (Athridas, Terenthis, Thundris, Raene `next=furbolgs`), dark_strand (Delgren `next=strand`).

**For the lead (World pack, outside my scope):** the voice checker fails two lines of `pool:guard_darnassus` (World): `g_short` ("Welcome. The branches are wide. You won't fall.") and `n_skyborne` ("Elves from the sky. Forgive me for staring. ..."). Suggested: "Welcome to Darnassus. The branches are wide, so you won't fall, whatever your stomach tells you." and "Elves from the sky... forgive me for staring, but a Sentinel is allowed to be curious once a century."

## Darkshore depth (Darkshore pack 1.2.0)

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Classic Auberdine: the Bravery to Menethil Harbor, the Moonspray to Rut'theran; Auberdine's Sentinels guard the Bravery's crossings; a mixed population including dwarves, gnomes and humans; dwarves keep a small smithy; murlocs threaten the fishing boats; the village council hires adventurers | [Auberdine](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Auberdine) |
| Thundris: Bashal'Aran overrun by demonic creatures (grells, satyrs) feeding on its magic, but they avoid one shrine with a blue aura (Asterion, a Highborne spirit glad of civil company after many years) | [Bashal'Aran (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Bashal%27Aran_(quest)) |
| Tools of the Highborne: Ameth'Aran to the south, Highborne spirits with ancient implements; Thundris "almost" feels the taint and has them destroyed | [Tools of the Highborne](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Tools_of_the_Highborne) |
| The Cliffspring River turning foul; Blackwood furbolgs up-river suspected but not the root; Felwood is the source, "one that I have seen in times past" | [The Cliffspring River](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Cliffspring_River) |
| The Blackwood Corrupted: a satyr corruptor's talisman; moonwell water and the furbolgs' food lure him out | [The Blackwood Corrupted](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Blackwood_Corrupted) |
| Seeking the Kor Gem: paladins sent by Jordan Stilwell; Thundris remembers him; Kor gems from the naga of Blackfathom Deeps; he purifies them; trusts the Silver Hand, not judging the order by one fallen member | [Seeking the Kor Gem](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Seeking_the_Kor_Gem); [Thundris Windweaver](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Thundris_Windweaver) |
| Terenthis: some of his brethren were rescued from a corrupt furbolg on Teldrassil; kill pathfinders and windtalkers south of Auberdine; "wash the blood... do not mourn"; Grimclaw signalled Volcor's danger; enchanted moonstalker cloak from fine pelts; Volcor wounded in a cave to the south (Blackwood Den) | [How Big a Threat?](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/How_Big_a_Threat%3F); [How Big a Threat? (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/How_Big_a_Threat%3F_(2)); [A Lost Master](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Lost_Master); [A Lost Master (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Lost_Master_(2)); [Volcor](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Volcor); [Escape Through Stealth](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Escape_Through_Stealth) |
| Gwennyth: sent by the Temple of the Moon; sea creatures beaching in growing numbers; Greymist murlocs feed on a giant thresher they could not have killed; remains sent to Darnassus by hippogryph; some think the creatures flee the waters around Teldrassil; the Temple gave her funds | [Washed Ashore](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Washed_Ashore); [Washed Ashore (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Washed_Ashore_(2)); [Beached Sea Creature (1)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Beached_Sea_Creature_(1)) |
| Glynda: moonkin once thought gentle, almost mystical; she keeps "a more realistic view"; the red crystal on the eastern range, maybe from Felwood; moonwell water revealed bone fragments and half a humanoid jawbone inside; she takes it to the council | [The Red Crystal](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Red_Crystal); [As Water Cascades](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/As_Water_Cascades); [The Fragments Within](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Fragments_Within) |
| Cerellean: woke from the long sleep, wandered in a daze to Ameth'Aran and saw Anaya's spirit; could not raise his hand against her | [For Love Eternal](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/For_Love_Eternal); [Cerellean Whiteclaw](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Cerellean_Whiteclaw) |
| The Master's Glaive: Onu says an "old, old lord of the earth" fell there; the Twilight's Hammer hold it in Classic | [Master's Glaive](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Master%27s_Glaive); [Darkshore (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Darkshore_(Classic)) |
| Classic road to Astranaar: Sentinel Selarin, Raene's subordinate, came for Terenthis and sends adventurers to Raene instead (Trek to Ashenvale) | [Trek to Ashenvale](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Trek_to_Ashenvale); [Sentinel Selarin](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Sentinel_Selarin) |
| The Explorers' League digs at Remtravel's Excavation | [Prospector Remtravel](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Prospector_Remtravel) |

**Wiki IDs seen (not used):** NPCs: Cerellean 3644, Volcor 3692, Grimclaw 3695, Sentinel Selarin 3694, Shaussiy 6737. Quests: Bashal'Aran 954, For Love Eternal 963, Tools of the Highborne 968, How Big a Threat? 984/985, A Lost Master 986/993, Escape Through Force 994, Escape Through Stealth 995, Seeking the Kor Gem 1442, Washed Ashore 3524/4681, Thundris Windweaver 4761, The Cliffspring River 4762, The Blackwood Corrupted 4763, The Red Crystal 4811, As Water Cascades 4812, The Fragments Within 4813, Trek to Ashenvale 990.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):** that the quest from Terenthis is titled exactly "Thundris Windweaver"; that Forever keeps the Bravery and the Moonspray. Thundris's Forever quests (Bloom of the Heavens (2), Expanding Horizons, Making Do, It's All Fun and Games Until...), Gwennyth's Holy Diver and the WANTED: Jai'vhanel poster have no wiki pages yet, so they aren't referenced.

**Year 1 notes:** Onward to Ashenvale was added in 2.0.3 and The Way to Auberdine is a draenei quest; neither is used, nor the Thundris-Raene rescue story from the former. Nothing of Auberdine's destruction, Lor'danel, or Cerellean and Grimclaw together.

**Authored, not canon:** see the "Depth pass (Darkshore 1.2.0)" section of `bibles/auberdine.md`.

World topics answered here (twilights_hammer already exists; fel_moss, furbolgs, sisterhood_of_elune proposed) are in `RPGDialogue_Darnassus/LORE_ADDITIONS.md`.


## Ashenvale depth (Ashenvale pack 1.2.0)

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Raene: hunter of the Sentinels; Korra (saber cat), Dagri (wolf); would rather face the Horde alone than watch them hack through the trees; Classic gossip: knows the forests as part of her, much danger hidden | [Raene Wolfrunner](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Raene_Wolfrunner) |
| Trek to Ashenvale: Sentinel Selarin sends you from Darkshore; Raene wishes you'd come under less dire circumstances and sends you to Astranaar's citizens | [Trek to Ashenvale](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Trek_to_Ashenvale) |
| An Aggressive Defense: furbolgs attacking travellers on the road east before the Falfarren River, camps south of the road; Culling the Threat: Thistlefur north of Astranaar grown greatly in months, chieftain Dal Bloodclaw | [An Aggressive Defense](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/An_Aggressive_Defense); [Culling the Threat](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Culling_the_Threat) |
| Raene's Cleansing: longtime friend Teronis sought a gem for the rod of Dartol, a dead evil wizard; found dead on the shrine island in Lake Falathim; "rest much better knowing his death was not in vain"; the rod lets one take another form to speak to a furbolg free of Fel Moss (Krolg); Ran Bloodtooth's skull and the rod put in a place of reverence | [Raene's Cleansing](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Raene%27s_Cleansing); [(2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Raene%27s_Cleansing_(2)); [(9)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Raene%27s_Cleansing_(9)); [(11)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Raene%27s_Cleansing_(11)) |
| Pelturas: Orendil Broadleaf's cure slowed the illness; Elune's Tear from the isle in Iris Lake washes away corruption; frosted dust from the Ruins of Stardust for a fever poultice; Pelturas and Relara used to gather moonstones at Fallen Sky Lake until the Shadethicket came; the Oracle hoards them | [Pelturas Whitemoon](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Pelturas_Whitemoon); [Orendil's Cure (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Orendil%27s_Cure_(Classic)); [Elune's Tear (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Elune%27s_Tear_(Classic)); [The Ruins of Stardust (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Ruins_of_Stardust_(quest)); [Fallen Sky Lake (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Fallen_Sky_Lake_(quest)) |
| Faldreas: Journey to Stonetalon Peak: the forest spirits say you're brave; Talondeep Path near Mystral Lake, Windshear Crag, Stonetalon Peak, Keeper Albagorm ("Faldreas has listened to the forest spirits") | [Journey to Stonetalon Peak](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Journey_to_Stonetalon_Peak); [Faldreas Goeth'Shael](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Faldreas_Goeth%27Shael) |
| Delgren: paladin at Maestra's Post; Balthule his apprentice, taught the Holy Light; the Dark Strand warlocks at the Tower of Althalaxx; Ilkrud Magthrull's scribblings; orc writing like a child's; his order scoffed when he learned Orcish, useful to understand enemies; unused to night elf manners at first, grown to respect them; the satyrs' blood-soaked temples; Feero carries supplies to Sentinel Starbreeze in Auberdine | [Delgren the Purifier](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Delgren_the_Purifier); [The Tower of Althalaxx (2)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Tower_of_Althalaxx_(2)) to [(6)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Tower_of_Althalaxx_(6)); [Supplies to Auberdine](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Supplies_to_Auberdine) |
| Senani (Classic, at Splintertree): the call of the hunt from deep within; Ursangous near the Talondeep Path west of Mystral Lake, easiest, a threat to travellers to Stonetalon; his paw's crescent suggests night elf training; Shadumbra pitch-black and fast, a danger on the way west to the Zoram Strand; Sharptalon the mightiest, terrorizing peons from the lumber camps | [Senani Thunderheart](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Senani_Thunderheart); [The Ashenvale Hunt (Ashenvale)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Ashenvale_Hunt_(Ashenvale)); [Ursangous's Paw (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Ursangous%27s_Paw_(quest)); [Shadumbra's Head (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Shadumbra%27s_Head_(Classic)); [Sharptalon's Claw (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Sharptalon%27s_Claw_(Classic)) |
| Mastok: Stonetalon Standstill: the lake by the Talondeep Path came alive (water elementals); transports to Stonetalon at a standstill; scout the gazebo on Mystral Lake facing the Alliance outpost (Silverwind Refuge); gossip "I don't like people" | [Stonetalon Standstill](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Stonetalon_Standstill); [Mastok Wrilehiss](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mastok_Wrilehiss) |
| Kuray'bin: outrunners from Silverwing Outpost sneak near Horde ground to gather intelligence; blinding the Alliance makes attacks less likely | [Ashenvale Outrunners (Classic)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Ashenvale_Outrunners_(Classic)); [Kuray'bin](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Kuray%27bin) |
| Splintertree: founded by Grom and the Warsong in the Third War; raids on Astranaar frequent | [Splintertree Post](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Splintertree_Post) |

**Wiki IDs seen (not used):** NPCs: Pelturas 3894, Relara 3892, Faldreas 3996, Senani 12696, Kuray'bin 12867. Quests: Trek to Ashenvale 990, Raene's Cleansing 991/1023/1024/1030/1046, An Aggressive Defense 1025, Culling the Threat 1054, Orendil's Cure (Classic) 1020, Elune's Tear (Classic) 1033, The Ruins of Stardust 1034, Fallen Sky Lake 1035, Journey to Stonetalon Peak 1056, The Tower of Althalaxx 965/966/967/970/973/1140/1167/981, Supplies to Auberdine 976, Stonetalon Standstill 25, Ashenvale Outrunners 6503, The Ashenvale Hunt (Ashenvale) 6383, Ursangous's Paw 23, Shadumbra's Head 24, Sharptalon's Claw 2, The Hunt Completed 247.

**Assumptions (confirm in game):** "Raene's Cleansing" is eleven quests with one title, so `questDone` fires after the first part; Raene's done line is worded to fit any point in the chain. Same for The Tower of Althalaxx (nine parts) and The Ashenvale Hunt (several start points). Senani stands at Splintertree in Classic (she moved to Silverwind Refuge in Cataclysm).

**Year 1 notes:** Onward to Ashenvale (2.0.3), Check in on the Edunes and The Befouled Element (later) are no longer used; their lines keep their ids under Classic quests (Trek to Ashenvale, Journey to Stonetalon Peak, Stonetalon Standstill). Delgren's company is not named: the wiki says Argent Dawn in one place and Knights of the Silver Hand in another. Nothing of Delgren's death, Maestra's Post falling, Silverwind Refuge as a Horde target, or Relara's relapse.

**Authored, not canon:** see the "Depth pass (Ashenvale 1.2.0)" section of `bibles/ashenvale.md`.

World topics answered here (warsong, furbolgs, dark_strand) are in `RPGDialogue_Darnassus/LORE_ADDITIONS.md`.

## Western Plaguelands (Western Plaguelands pack 1.0.0)

Classic facts; levels 50–60. The Bulwark stands on the Tirisfal side of the border, so the pack loads in Tirisfal Glades too. Quest IDs come from capture only.

| Fact used | Source |
|---|---|
| Chillwind Camp: Alliance camp at the southern entrance, a small Argent Dawn contingent, few troops, mercenaries for strikes on Andorhal and the cauldrons; Shadowbreaker sends paladins to Valorfist; Classic NPCs incl. Arbington, Pureheart, Bibilfaz, Valorfist, MacDonnell, Nathaniel Dumah, Weldon Barov | [Chillwind Camp](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Chillwind_Camp); [Chillwind Camp (quest)](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Chillwind_Camp_(quest)) |
| The Bulwark: the Forsaken's last defense against the Scourge on the Tirisfal border, with the Argent Dawn; Uther's camp for Capital City's survivors in the Scourging; a wall of debris that stops roaming zombies (RPG) | [The Bulwark](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Bulwark) |
| Commander Ashlam Valorfist: sent by Stormwind against the Scourge in Andorhal; MacDonnell his second; Clear the Way (Sorrow Hill, the east road), All Along the Watchtowers (beacon torch, four towers), Alas, Andorhal (Araj the Summoner), Mission Accomplished! | [Commander Ashlam Valorfist](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Commander_Ashlam_Valorfist); [Clear the Way](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Clear_the_Way_(Western_Plaguelands)); [All Along the Watchtowers](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/All_Along_the_Watchtowers_(Alliance)); [Alas, Andorhal](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Alas,_Andorhal_(Alliance_Classic)) |
| The plague cauldrons: eight across Lordaeron, four in the west (Felstone Field, Dalson's Tears, the Writhing Haunt, Gahrron's Withering); too strong to shut down, so the counter agent turns them on the Scourge for a day or so; cauldron lords hold the keys; vitreous focuser, arcane quickener (thirty minutes), runecloth and parts of the undead | [The Scourge Cauldrons](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Scourge_Cauldrons_(Alliance)); [High Priestess MacDonnell](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/High_Priestess_MacDonnell); [Return to the Bulwark](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Return_to_the_Bulwark) |
| Alchemist Arbington and Apothecary Dithers: the Scholomance key chain (Skeletal Fragments, Mold Rhymes With... to Krinkle Goodsteel in Gadgetzan, Fire Plume Forged, Araj's Scarab, The Key to Scholomance); Araj ran the Scholomance before Andorhal; Dithers's knee | [Alchemist Arbington](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Alchemist_Arbington); [Apothecary Dithers](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Apothecary_Dithers); [Araj's Scarab](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Araj%27s_Scarab_(Alliance)); [Mold Rhymes With...](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mold_Rhymes_With..._(Alliance)) |
| Weldon and Alexi Barov: the Barovs bargained with Kel'Thuzad; Weldon escaped Caer Darrow and spared his undead brother; both want the four deeds (Caer Darrow, Brill, Tarren Mill, Southshore) and each other's head | [Weldon Barov](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Weldon_Barov); [Alexi Barov](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Alexi_Barov); [Barov Family Fortune](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Barov_Family_Fortune_(Alliance)); [The Last Barov](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Last_Barov_(Horde)) |
| Argent Officers Pureheart (left the Silver Hand with Tyrosus; sent Gregor Greystone to Everlook) and Garush (orc, at the Bulwark): the Argent Dawn Commission and scourgestones | [Argent Officer Pureheart](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Argent_Officer_Pureheart); [Argent Officer Garush](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Argent_Officer_Garush) |
| Nathaniel Dumah: his father ran the Northridge Lumber Mill; the family fled to Stormwind; the Crusade holds the mill; A Plague Upon Thee (Plaguewood termites) | [Nathaniel Dumah](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Nathaniel_Dumah); [A Plague Upon Thee](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Plague_Upon_Thee_(Alliance)) |
| High Executor Derrington and Shadow Priestess Vandis: the Horde's cauldron and Andorhal chain; Scarlet Diversions (burn the Crusade's tent between Felstone Field and Dalson's Tears, plant a Scourge banner) | [High Executor Derrington](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/High_Executor_Derrington); [Scarlet Diversions](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Scarlet_Diversions) |
| Janice Felstone: ghost at Felstone Field calling for her husband John (a Jabbering Ghoul on the farm); sister of Ol' Emma in Stormwind; Better Late Than Never, Good Luck Charm, Two Halves Become One | [Janice Felstone](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Janice_Felstone); [Two Halves Become One](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Two_Halves_Become_One) |
| Mulgris Deepriver: dying tauren druid of the Cenarion Circle at the Writhing Haunt; The Wildlife Suffers Too; dead after his last quest | [Mulgris Deepriver](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mulgris_Deepriver); [The Wildlife Suffers Too](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/The_Wildlife_Suffers_Too) |
| Marlene Redpath: ghost at Sorrow Hill; hid her niece Pamela at Darrowshire; Little Pamela, Auntie Marlene, A Strange Historian (Joseph's ring to Chromie in Andorhal) | [Marlene Redpath](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Marlene_Redpath); [Little Pamela](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Little_Pamela_(Classic)); [A Strange Historian](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/A_Strange_Historian_(Classic)) |

| Eva Sarkhoff: the Barovs' head maid, wife of the butler Lucien; hid six months, then tortured by Krastinov and fed to ghouls; Krastinov, Krastinov's Bag of Horrors (Jandice Barov), Kirtonos the Herald | [Eva Sarkhoff](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Eva_Sarkhoff); [Krastinov's Bag of Horrors](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Krastinov%27s_Bag_of_Horrors_(quest)) |
| "Argent Defender" names are guards (the existing `defender$` pattern) | [Chillwind Camp](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Chillwind_Camp) |

### Western Plaguelands depth pass (pack 1.0.0, released in 0.31.0)
- Andorhal was the distribution center for agricultural supplies in Lordaeron's northern provinces; Kel'Thuzad's cult shipped plagued grain from it. https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Andorhal
- Janice Felstone's parcel goes to her sister, Ol' Emma in Stormwind, who believed her family died in the plague. https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Better_Late_Than_Never_(Classic), https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Good_Natured_Emma
- Marlene Redpath is Joseph Redpath's sister; Pamela is his daughter; Joseph died in the Battle of Darrowshire. https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Joseph_Redpath
- Caer Darrow, an island keep on Darrowmere Lake; the Barovs dealt with Kel'Thuzad to keep fortune and lands, and the keep became the Scholomance. https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Caer_Darrow
- Hearthglen lies north of Andorhal, held by the Scarlet Crusade from Mardenholde Keep. https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Hearthglen
- Mulgris Deepriver's Classic gossip asks Alliance races to set differences aside; he dies after The Wildlife Suffers Too (2). https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Mulgris_Deepriver
- Authored (not canon) details are listed in RPGDialogue_WesternPlaguelands/bibles/westernplaguelands.md, "Authored in the depth pass".
